The framework decomposes the 3D-QA task into four interpretable components: (1) question-driven multi-view selection, (2) guided visual grounding with language-conditioned object cues, (3) spatial context encoding via a bird's-eye-view (BEV) viewpoint indicator, and (4) structured answer generation through role-based reasoning. This design enables structured, robust, and interpretable reasoning without requiring model tuning. Experimental results on ScanQA and SQA3D show that ViewMind3D achieves competitive performance compared to prior training-free and fine-tuned 3D-LLMs. In particular, our method improves performance on spatially grounded question types, such as ``What'' questions in SQA3D, while maintaining strong overall accuracy (50.8\%) and achieving 73.4 CIDEr on ScanQA. These results demonstrate that effective 3D reasoning can be achieved through modular orchestration of general-purpose LLMs and VLMs for robotic perception in real-world environments.

</details>


### [90] [VisualRouter: Query-Grounded Visual Sampling for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28463)
*Haiyue Zhang,Yi Bin,Xun Jiang,Zeyu Ma,Duo Peng,Guoqing Wang,Yang Yang,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisualRouter是一个无需训练、即插即用的查询引导视觉采样框架，先对查询进行分类（全局或局部），再针对性地采用不同的帧采样策略，在有限帧数下平衡相关性、覆盖度和多样性，显著提升多个LVLM在长视频理解任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解中，大量视觉token和有限的上下文窗口使得现有方法难以有效处理。现有视觉采样方法要么依赖相关性采样导致帧选择冗余、时间覆盖不足，要么采用固定采样策略无法适配不同类型的查询（如全局查询与局部查询）。需要一个能根据查询类型自适应调整采样策略的解决方案。

Method: VisualRouter首先将每个查询分类为全局或局部类型。对于全局查询，采用相关性-覆盖度混合策略，在保留时间覆盖的同时维持查询相关的视觉证据。对于局部查询，采用事件感知的帧选择策略，包括事件划分、片段级帧分配和事件内帧选择三个步骤，在有限的输入帧数下联合平衡相关性、覆盖度和多样性。

Result: 在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和MLVU三个基准上，使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B时分别相比均匀采样提升5.2%、7.7%和11.6%，并且在相同设置下优于现有的无需训练的视觉采样方法。

Conclusion: VisualRouter通过查询类型感知的差异化采样策略，有效解决了长视频理解中帧采样的适应性问题，无需训练即可即插即用地提升多种LVLM在长视频任务上的表现，证明了查询引导的框架设计在多模态视频理解中的有效性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have achieved significant progress in video understanding, yet understanding long videos remains challenging due to the large number of visual tokens and limited context windows. Visual sampling provides a practical solution by selecting an informative subset of frames. However, existing methods typically either rely on relevance-aware sampling, leading to redundant frame selection and insufficient temporal coverage, or adopt a fixed sampling strategy regardless of query type. In this paper, we propose VisualRouter, a training-free and plug-and-play framework for query-grounded visual sampling. VisualRouter first classifies each query as either global or local and then applies the corresponding sampling strategy. For global queries, it employs a relevance-coverage hybrid strategy that preserves temporal coverage while retaining query-relevant visual evidence. For local queries, it adopts an event-aware frame selection strategy that performs event partitioning, segment-level frame allocation, and intra-event frame selection, jointly balancing relevance, coverage, and diversity with a limited number of input frames. Experiments show that VisualRouter consistently improves multiple LVLMs over uniform sampling, achieving gains of 5.2%, 7.7%, and 11.6% on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU with Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and outperforming existing training-free visual sampling methods under the same setting.

</details>


### [91] [Can Vision-Language Models Reason about AI Edits in Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28464)
*Darsha Udayanga,Pin-Yu Chen,Payel Das,Qiang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于GRPO强化学习的框架，训练视觉语言模型（VLM）对AI篡改图像进行结构化推理，并通过轻量级分割模型生成像素级定位掩码，在不依赖显式推理监督的情况下实现与最先进伪造检测器相媲美的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像的篡改检测对可信AI至关重要，但传统二分类器缺乏可解释性和泛化能力。现有基于VLM的方法依赖监督微调和策划好的解释，未充分利用VLM固有的推理能力，因此需要探索一种无需显式推理监督的训练方式。

Method: 提出基于GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）的训练框架，利用简单的准确率和格式奖励激励VLM生成思考轨迹后再给出篡改判断。模型首先对输入图像产生结构化推理轨迹并预测是否被篡改，随后由轻量级分割模型依据推理输出生成像素级定位掩码。同时引入统一的评估指标eff-IoU来联合评估检测和定位性能。

Result: 在多个图像篡改数据集上的实验表明，该方法在检测和定位性能上与最先进的图像伪造检测器相当，且所需的监督信号显著更弱。

Conclusion: 强化学习为教导VLM推理AI生成内容提供了一种有效且可扩展的机制，即使在监督较弱的情况下也能达到竞争性性能，并提出了统一定量化检测与定位的eff-IoU指标。

Abstract: Detection and localization of AI-tampered images are critical for trustworthy AI, yet modern generative models have made such manipulations increasingly difficult to identify. While traditional binary classifiers can detect image tampering, they lack interpretability and generalization. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer a promising alternative due to their strong visual understanding and reasoning capabilities; however, existing approaches typically rely on supervised finetuning with curated explanations rather than exploiting their inherent reasoning capabilities. In this work, we investigate whether VLMs can be trained to reason about AI-generated image edits using reinforcement learning (RL) rather than explicit reasoning supervision. Motivated by the success in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), an RL technique that incentivizes the model to reason by generating thinking traces prior to giving the final answer, we propose a GRPO-based training framework that utilizes simple accuracy and format rewards. Given an input image, the model produces a structured reasoning trace and predicts whether the image has been tampered with. A lightweight segmentation model is then guided by the reasoning output to generate pixel-level localization masks. Experiments across multiple image manipulation datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive detection and localization performance compared to state-of-the-art image forgery detectors, despite requiring substantially weaker supervision. We introduce effective intersection over union (eff-IoU), a unified metric to jointly evaluate detection and localization. These results suggest that reinforcement learning provides an effective and scalable mechanism for teaching VLMs to reason about AI-generated content.

</details>


### [92] [Towards Real-Time PixOOD: Efficient Anomaly Segmentation for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28483)
*Luca de Martino,Federico Aromolo,Federico Nesti,Giorgio Buttazzo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的实时异常分割流水线，通过重构PixOOD的Neyman-Pearson评分阶段并利用TensorRT硬件优化编译，在桌面GPU和嵌入式平台上分别达到182 FPS和75 FPS，相比原始基线提速20倍和18倍，实现了在自动驾驶和铁路场景中的板载实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时异常分割对自动驾驶系统的安全性至关重要，但现有方法虽精度高，计算成本过大，难以在嵌入式硬件上部署。作者旨在解决高级异常分割方法在资源受限平台上部署性能不足的问题。

Method: 该方法包括两个关键步骤：(1) 重构PixOOD（当前最先进的分布外检测方法）中的Neyman-Pearson评分阶段，以简化计算流程；(2) 通过硬件优化的TensorRT编译部署完整流水线，同时针对嵌入式（Jetson AGX Orin）和桌面（RTX 4060）平台进行适配，覆盖自动驾驶和铁路两个应用领域。

Result: 在桌面NVIDIA RTX 4060 GPU上达到182 FPS，在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin嵌入式平台上达到75 FPS，分别比原始基线快20倍和18倍，同时保持先进的异常分割精度。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过算法重构与硬件优化编译相结合，先进的异常分割方法能够高效部署于自动驾驶和铁路应用的嵌入式平台，满足实时板载处理的需求。

Abstract: Real-time anomaly segmentation is essential for the safety of autonomous systems. Although recent approaches offer high accuracy, their computational cost limits their deployment on embedded hardware. This work presents an efficient and accelerated pipeline designed for both embedded and desktop platforms, targeting the autonomous driving and railway domains. The proposed approach reformulates the Neyman-Pearson scoring stage of PixOOD, a state-of-the-art out-of-distribution detection method, and deploys the full pipeline through hardware-optimized TensorRT compilation, reaching up to 182 FPS on a desktop NVIDIA RTX 4060 GPU and 75 FPS on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin embedded platform, respectively 20x and 18x faster than the original baseline. The achieved results demonstrate that advanced anomaly segmentation can be efficiently deployed for onboard processing in autonomous driving and railway applications.

</details>


### [93] [AuricularWorld: Hierarchical Action-Guided World Modeling for Fine-Grained Auricular Structure Segmentation from CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28487)
*Jingwen Yang,Senmao Wang,Luoyao Kang,Runmeng Cui,Keying Zhang,Yunjia Bao,Haifan Gong,Lin Lin,Haiyue Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于世界模型的CT耳廓结构分割框架，通过在编码器-解码器架构的中间潜空间中引入确定性循环状态空间模型，实现迭代式解剖推理。该方法在多尺度特征融合初始化潜动态的基础上，推理时执行三步潜空间展开，并通过分层解剖动作逐步优化潜表示，最终显著提升小尺寸、不规则、重叠的耳廓结构分割精度，HD95降低超过43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耳廓结构在CT图像中体积小、软骨边界高度不规则、软骨与周围软组织界面模糊，且临床标注常包含嵌套重叠的复合结构（如软骨+皮肤）与纯软骨区域，传统前馈式分割模型难以有效处理这些挑战。

Method: 提出基于世界模型的分割框架：1) 采用编码器-解码器架构；2) 在中间潜空间引入确定性循环状态空间模型；3) 将多尺度编码器特征与部分解码表示融合形成结构观测，用于初始化潜动态；4) 推理时执行无真实标签引导的三步潜空间展开；5) 通过分层解剖动作更新循环状态并渐进细化潜表示；6) 潜轨迹投影回解码器并与高分辨率特征结合生成最终分割；7) 提出平衡分层动作目标函数，解决前景稀疏、缺失解剖组、增删操作不平衡问题。

Result: 在CT耳廓结构分割任务上，所提框架在分割精度上持续提升，HD95（95%豪斯多夫距离）降低超过43%，尤其对小型、不规则及重叠的耳廓结构效果显著。

Conclusion: 潜空间世界模型推理对具有挑战性的医学图像分割任务（如耳廓精细结构分割）是有效的，展示了超越传统前馈预测的迭代解剖推理能力。

Abstract: Fine-grained segmentation of auricular structures in CT is challenging because the ear occupies a small image region, cartilage boundaries are highly irregular, and interfaces between cartilage and surrounding soft tissues are often ambiguous. Clinical annotations may also include both composite structures containing cartilage and adjacent skin and their corresponding cartilage-only regions, producing nested and overlapping labels. We propose a world-model-based segmentation framework that enables iterative anatomical reasoning beyond conventional feed-forward prediction. Built on an encoder-decoder architecture, the framework introduces a deterministic recurrent state-space model into the intermediate latent space. Multi-scale encoder features and partially decoded representations are fused to form a structural observation that initializes the latent dynamics. During inference, the model performs a three-step latent rollout without ground-truth guidance. Hierarchical anatomical actions update the recurrent state and progressively refine the latent representation. The resulting latent trajectory is projected back into the decoder and combined with high-resolution features to produce the final segmentation. To learn reliable latent transitions, we introduce a balanced hierarchical action objective that addresses foreground sparsity, missing anatomical groups, and imbalance between add and remove operations. Extensive experiments show that the proposed framework consistently improves segmentation accuracy and reduces HD95 by more than 43% for small, irregular, and overlapping auricular structures in CT. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of latent world-model reasoning for challenging medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [94] [RefCaptioner: Multi-Reference Image-Grounded Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28509)
*Tengfei Liu,Yang Shi,Yuran Wang,Xiaohan Zhang,Yuqing Wen,Yuqi Tang,Qixun Wang,Zhuoran Zhang,Xuanyu Zhu,Weihong Lin,Xinlei Yu,Yujie Wei,Xinwei Long,Fengxiang Wang,Xinlong Chen,Yue Ding,Jialu Chen,Haotian Wang,Yuanxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多参考图像视频描述生成任务及RefCaptioner框架，通过两阶段后训练（混合数据SFT + 层次覆盖折扣GRPO）实现短语级参考绑定与干扰项拒绝，并构建了包含20,000个视频和171,354张参考图像的数据集及MRVBench评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频描述模型虽能生成自然语言描述，但无法将视频中的局部视觉元素显式关联到多张参考图像，缺乏细粒度的跨模态参考定位能力。

Method: 提出RefCaptioner，采用两阶段后训练框架：第一阶段使用混合数据进行有监督微调（SFT），第二阶段引入层次覆盖折扣GRPO（Hierarchical Coverage-Discounted GRPO）强化学习，联合优化参考选择、短语级绑定、干扰项拒绝和跨参考一致性，同时保持通用视频描述能力。

Result: 在开源模型中取得最佳综合性能，且在标准视频描述基准上保持竞争力；人工评测显示其描述更受标注者青睐，并能支持开源及专有视频生成器实现更忠实于源的视频重建。

Conclusion: 多参考图像视频描述是可行且有价值的新任务，RefCaptioner验证了两阶段后训练范式的有效性，为视频-图像联合理解与生成提供新方向。

Abstract: Existing video captioning models generate natural descriptions of video content but cannot explicitly ground local visual elements to multiple reference images. We introduce multi-reference image-grounded video captioning, a new task requiring factual video descriptions with phrase-level reference grounding, and propose RefCaptioner, a two-stage post-training framework for this task. RefCaptioner combines mixed-data SFT with Hierarchical Coverage-Discounted GRPO to jointly improve reference selection, phrase-level binding, distractor rejection, and cross-reference consistency while preserving general video-captioning ability. To support training, we construct a corpus containing $20,000$ videos and 171,354 reference images. We further introduce MRVBench, a benchmark for evaluating caption factuality and multi-reference grounding on both real-world and AI-generated videos. Experiments show that RefCaptioner achieves the best overall performance among the open-source models while remaining competitive on standard video captioning benchmarks. Human evaluation further confirms that its captions are preferred by annotators and enable more source-faithful video reconstruction with both open-source and proprietary video generators.

</details>


### [95] [Beyond Frame Selection: Generative Latent Evidence Aggregation for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28516)
*Bowen Liu,Shuning Wang,Xinpeng Ding,Zhiheng Wu,Bodong Du,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GenEvA，一种基于分布引导的潜证据聚合框架，用于长视频理解中在帧选择后进行查询相关的跨帧证据整合，以极小token开销显著提升视频MLLM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解管道将视频压缩为少量帧或视觉token用于答案生成，这些管道虽聚焦保留相关视觉内容作为显式证据，但仅仅使证据可用并不能保证跨时刻的互补线索被整合用于回答。因此需要一种在帧选择后组织查询相关的跨帧证据的方法。

Method: GenEvA将帧选择后的阶段形式化为潜证据接口，并实例化为生成式潜证据聚合框架。具体地，GenEvA使用查询条件证据分布来聚焦对相关帧的聚合，从帧特定信息中形成紧凑的跨帧潜证据。由于跨帧整合并非总是必要，同一分布决定是否插入该潜补充（即自适应证据调用，Adaptive Evidence Invocation）。

Result: 在四个基准和两种Video-MLLM骨干上，GenEvA持续超越匹配帧基线。在8帧设置下，四个基准的LLaVA-Video平均提升+5.2点，Qwen2.5-VL在LVBench上准确率提升+10.1点。这些增益仅需0.11%至0.40%的平均视频token开销。分析进一步展示了任务感知分配和自适应证据调用的优势。

Conclusion: GenEvA证明了在压缩视频管道中引入查询相关的跨帧潜证据聚合可以显著提升长视频理解能力，且增加的计算开销极小。任务感知的证据分配和自适应的证据调用机制是提升性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Long-video understanding commonly compresses videos into a small set of frames or visual tokens for answer generation. Existing compact pipelines focus on retaining relevant visual content as explicit evidence. Yet making evidence available does not ensure that complementary cues across moments are integrated for answering. Our key idea is to organize selected frames into query-relevant cross-frame evidence before generation. We formulate this post-selection stage as a latent evidence interface and instantiate it with GenEvA ($\textbf{Gen}erative$ $Latent$ $\textbf{Ev}idence$ $\textbf{A}ggregation$), a distribution-guided latent evidence aggregation framework. Specifically, GenEvA uses a query-conditioned evidence distribution to focus aggregation on relevant frames, forming compact cross-frame latent evidence from their frame-specific information. Since cross-frame integration is not always needed, the same distribution determines whether to insert this latent complement. Across four benchmarks and two Video-MLLM backbones, GenEvA consistently improves matched-frame baselines. At 8 frames, it raises the four-benchmark LLaVA-Video average by $+5.2$ points and Qwen2.5-VL accuracy on LVBench by $+10.1$ points. These gains require only $0.11\%$--$0.40\%$ average video-token overhead; analyses further show task-aware allocation and benefits from Adaptive Evidence Invocation.

</details>


### [96] [What to Remove, What to Preserve: Dual-Ambiguity Rectification for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28526)
*Cencen Liu,Wen Yin,Dongyang Zhang,Dongmin Li,Shan Zhao,Bing Su,Tao He,Jielei Wang,Guoming Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAR-Net，一种用于全合一图像复原的网络，通过双歧义修正机制（语义歧义与空间歧义）解决多退化条件下共享潜在空间中退化线索与场景内容纠缠的问题，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全合一图像复原方法将异质退化条件编码在共享潜在空间中，导致退化相关线索与场景内容相互纠缠，造成"双重歧义"：通道级调制中的语义歧义和复原响应中的空间歧义，进而引发内容损坏和残留伪影。

Method: DAR-Net包含三个核心模块：(1) 退化原型表示（DAR）模块，通过单纯形约束的原型混合建模构建结构化退化状态；(2) 语义歧义修正（SeAR）模块，基于退化状态生成退化感知提示以改进解码器的通道级条件化；(3) 空间歧义修正（SpAR）模块，将退化感知特征与互补特征正则化为正交响应子空间，减少去除与保留线索间的空间干扰。

Result: 在标准全合一复原基准上，DAR-Net在三种退化与五种退化设置下均取得最佳整体性能，平均PSNR分别比最强竞争者提高0.14 dB和0.34 dB；在CDD-11和WeatherBench上也展示了优越性能。

Conclusion: DAR-Net通过分别修正语义与空间层面的歧义，有效解耦了退化信息与场景内容，在多种退化场景下实现了更优的全合一图像复原效果。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration aims to handle diverse degradations within a unified framework. Existing methods commonly encode heterogeneous degradation conditions in a shared latent space, where degradation-related cues and scene content can remain entangled. We characterize the resulting challenge as dual ambiguity: semantic ambiguity in channel-wise modulation and spatial ambiguity in restoration responses, which can lead to content corruption and residual artifacts. To mitigate this issue, we propose DAR-Net, a Dual-Ambiguity Rectification Network for all-in-one image restoration. DAR-Net first introduces a Degradation Archetype Representation (DAR) module to construct a structured degradation state through simplex-constrained archetype mixture modeling. Based on this state, a Semantic Ambiguity Rectification (SeAR) module generates degradation-aware prompts to improve channel-wise conditioning in the decoder. A Spatial Ambiguity Rectification (SpAR) module further regularizes degradation-aware and complementary features toward orthogonal response subspaces, reducing spatial interference between removal and preservation cues. Extensive experiments on standard all-in-one restoration benchmarks show that DAR-Net achieves the best overall performance under both three-degradation and five-degradation settings, improving the average PSNR over the strongest competitor by 0.14 dB and 0.34 dB, respectively; it additionally shows superior performance on CDD-11 and WeatherBench.

</details>


### [97] [MarkushGlyph and OCSRGlyph: Improved Chemical Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28532)
*Alex Andonian,Samuel G Rodriques,Andrew D White,Siddharth M Narayanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OCSRGlyph和MarkushGlyph两个模型，将光学化学结构识别（OCSR）和Markush结构解析统一为图像到文本的翻译任务。OCSRGlyph通过精细处理立体化学提升单分子识别精度，MarkushGlyph作为视觉语言模型直接从图像读取整个Markush结构，避免此前多阶段流程的复杂性。此外，作者还提出一种新的Markush结构翻译准确率评估指标，以解决现有指标失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学结构在专利和科学文献中以图像形式存在，为程序化使用（如数据库索引或构建机器学习训练集）需将其转换为线性表示。单分子识别（OCSR）技术已较成熟，但Markush结构（表示一组分子家族）的解析仍具挑战性。现有Markush解析系统多采用多阶段流程分别处理视觉和文本内容，复杂且易出错，且现有评估指标存在失效模式。

Method: 1) 将OCSR和Markush结构解析统一建模为图像到文本的翻译问题；2) 提出OCSRGlyph模型，通过仔细考虑立体化学信息提升单分子识别性能；3) 提出MarkushGlyph视觉语言模型，将整个Markush结构作为图像输入直接读取，对比现有方法的多阶段处理流程；4) 引入新的评估指标用于Markush结构翻译准确率测定，解决先前指标的失效模式。

Result: OCSRGlyph在OCSR任务上达到最先进性能，通过立体化学处理超越此前方法；MarkushGlyph能够将整个Markush结构作为图像读取；新提出的评估指标能够处理先前指标中存在的失效模式。

Conclusion: 将化学结构识别统一为图像到文本翻译任务可同时提升单分子和Markush结构的解析效果。基于视觉语言模型的端到端方法在多阶段系统之外提供了更简洁有效的方案，配合新的评估指标，为Markush结构自动解析提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Chemical structures appear in patents and the scientific literature as images. For programmatic usage, such as indexing in databases or constructing machine learning model training sets, they must be transformed into line notations. The two common forms of this task are translating an image of a single molecule (optical chemical structure recognition - OCSR) and translating a Markush structure that represents a family of molecules. While prior work in the former case is quite mature, Markush structure parsing remains a challenging task. In this work, we treat both tasks as an image-to-text translation problem. We then propose OCSRGlyph, a state-of-the-art OCSR model, improving performance over prior methods by carefully considering stereochemistry. For the Markush task, we introduce MarkushGlyph, a vision-language model that reads the entire Markush structure as an image. This contrasts with prior systems, which often use multiple stages to separately process visual and text input content. Finally, we introduce a new metric for determining the accuracy of Markush structure translations, handling failure modes present in prior metrics.

</details>


### [98] [ScaFE: Data-Efficient Scar Classification with LLM-Generated Clinical Feature Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28538)
*Ruman Wang,Hangting Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ScaFE框架，将大型语言模型的临床知识转化为可执行的特征程序，用于病理性疤痕（瘢痕疙瘩与增生性疤痕）的图像分类。通过本地执行程序、仅回传聚合统计信息，ScaFE在600张三医院图像上实现81.0%的站点宏平衡准确率，比最强基线BiomedCLIP高出10.0个百分点，且具备数据高效性、跨站点泛化性和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床上区分瘢痕疙瘩与增生性疤痕需要依赖专家标注数据，但专家标注数据稀缺，且不同医院间的图像采集差异大。端到端图像模型数据依赖性强，而将照片发送至云端视觉语言模型（VLM）可能违反本地数据治理要求，且决策难以复现和审计。因此需要一种既利用大语言模型知识、又能本地执行且可审计的分类方法。

Method: ScaFE的核心创新是将LLM的临床知识转化为确定性、可执行的"特征程序"而非直接让模型诊断图像。具体流程为：(1) 使用联网LLM检索临床证据并合成可度量视觉疤痕属性的程序；(2) 候选程序在受限本地环境中执行，仅返回聚合验证统计量和特征级SHAP摘要用于迭代修复和优化；(3) 原始图像和患者级输出始终保留在本地；(4) 最终用轻量级随机森林对结构化特征表示进行分类。

Result: 在三家医院600张照片的留一站点验证中，ScaFE达到81.0%的站点宏平衡准确率，超越最强基线BiomedCLIP 10.0个百分点。仅使用10%开发数据时，ScaFE仍保持72.0%平衡准确率，领先基线11.8个百分点。迭代优化将可执行程序率从66.7%提升至95.0%，91.7%的最终特征具有验证证据。

Conclusion: ScaFE证明了LLM知识可以通过本地、可审计的特征程序支持数据高效、跨站点的医学图像分类，而非依赖直接的VLM决策。该方法在准确率、数据效率和可审计性之间取得了良好平衡，为隐私敏感的医疗图像分类提供了新范式。

Abstract: Classifying pathological scars from clinical photographs requires distinguishing keloids from hypertrophic scars despite limited expert-labeled data and substantial acquisition variation across hospitals. End-to-end image models remain data-dependent, whereas sending photographs to a hosted vision-language model (VLM) may conflict with local data-governance requirements and yields decisions that are difficult to reproduce and audit. We introduce ScaFE (Scar Feature Engineering), which transfers clinical knowledge from a large language model (LLM) into deterministic, executable feature programs instead of asking the model to diagnose images. A web-enabled LLM retrieves clinical evidence and synthesizes programs that measure visually assessable scar attributes. Candidate programs execute in a restricted local environment, and only aggregate validation statistics and feature-level SHAP summaries are returned for iterative repair and refinement; raw images and patient-level outputs remain local. A lightweight Random Forest then operates on the resulting structured representation. On 600 photographs from three hospitals under leave-one-site-out evaluation, ScaFE achieves 81.0% site-macro balanced accuracy, exceeding the strongest baseline, BiomedCLIP, by 10.0 percentage points. With only 10% of the development data, ScaFE retains 72.0% balanced accuracy and an 11.8-point lead. Iterative refinement also raises the executable-program rate from 66.7% to 95.0%, with verified evidence for 91.7% of the final features. These results show that LLM knowledge can support data-efficient, cross-site medical image classification through local and auditable feature programs rather than direct VLM decisions.

</details>


### [99] [MIND: Multimodal Intent-Driven Network via Diffusion Transformers for Medical Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28565)
*Yunzhan Fu,Xiangyu Shen,Yifei Sun,Yuhan Chen,Jian Wu,Hongxia Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MIND框架，一种基于扩散Transformer的多模态意图驱动网络，通过BiomedGPT生成意图驱动文本引导医学图像融合过程，并解决DiT在2D空间连续性丢失和语义漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像融合方法采用全局统一融合规则，缺乏对诊断意图和病理结构的深入理解，无法针对特定临床诊断需求进行灵活融合。

Method: 1) 利用BioMedGPT从源图像生成意图驱动融合文本，以病理感知的诊断意图指导融合过程；2) 设计多尺度潜在适配器（Multi-scale Latent Adapter），在序列化之前显式提取源图像特征并通过严格的维度对齐注入网络，克服DiT中1D序列展平导致的2D空间连续性损失；3) 设计医学语义一致性损失（medical semantic consistency loss），确保融合图像与融合文本之间的深层语义锁定，同时保持底层物理流形重建的稳定性。

Result: 在Harvard、BraTS和GFP数据集上的综合实验表明，MIND在融合质量上优于现有方法，显著提升了下游脑肿瘤分割准确率，并支持灵活的交互式融合。

Conclusion: MIND验证了意图驱动的医学图像融合范式在智能临床决策支持系统中的重要潜力，是一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Medical image fusion aims to integrate complementary information from diverse imaging modalities to support clinical diagnosis. Existing methods typically apply uniform fusion rules globally, lacking a deep understanding of diagnostic intents and pathological structures. To address these limitations, we propose MIND, a Multimodal Intent-Driven Network via Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for medical image fusion. Specifically, we utilize BioMedGPT to generate intent-driven fusion texts from source images, guiding the fusion process with pathology-aware diagnostic intents. To combat the loss of 2D spatial continuity caused by 1D sequence flattening in DiTs, we design a Multi-scale Latent Adapter. This module explicitly extracts source image features before serialization, injecting them into the network via strict dimensional alignment to effectively supplement image features. To resolve the semantic shift caused by decoupling image outputs from diagnostic intents, we design a medical semantic consistency loss. This loss ensures deep semantic locking between fused images and fusion texts while maintaining the stability of the underlying physical manifold reconstruction. Comprehensive experiments on the Harvard, BraTS, and GFP datasets reveal that MIND delivers superior fusion quality, significantly improves downstream brain tumor segmentation accuracy, and enables flexible interactive fusion, holding significant promise for intent-driven intelligent clinical decision support systems.

</details>


### [100] [Finding Change in Satellite Archives from Text: How to Combine Before-and-After Images Efficiently](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28571)
*Simon Roy,Mark Bong,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对双时序卫星图像变化检索中的融合模块进行了受控比较研究。使用固定CLIP编码器和统一训练配方，评估了三大类共八种融合设计（注意力、状态空间模型Mamba、以及提出的时序瓶颈融合TBF）。研究发现：训练免的二级搜索策略可将查询成本降低10-15倍且保持检索性能；Mamba的线性扫描在视觉Transformer典型patch数下并无速度优势；TBF压缩可减少2.3倍参数和1.6倍延迟，但过度压缩会损失变化相关细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测操作需要支持自然语言变化检索（如"找到出现新建筑的图像对"），这要求对大量前后时相图像对进行排序。融合模块作为检索核心组件，需在查询时对多个候选对运行，其速度直接决定搜索成本。然而现有研究缺乏对融合模块设计的系统比较，本文旨在通过受控实验解答如何最优构建该模块。

Method: 使用固定的冻结CLIP图像编码器，统一所有变体的训练配方，设计三大类共八种融合方案：注意力机制融合、状态空间模型（Mamba）融合、以及作者提出的时序瓶颈融合（TBF，学习压缩融合表示）。所有方案在LEVIR-CC和Dubai-CC两个基准上使用十个随机种子测试，保证统计显著性。同时验证了训练免的两阶段搜索策略：先用廉价差异模型短名单筛选，再用注意力融合精排序。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）训练免的两阶段搜索在LEVIR-CC上匹配或超过全融合召回率，查询成本降低10-15倍，Dubai-CC上R@1/R@5相当；（2）Mamba的线性扫描在L=196的patch数下无速度优势，内存带宽是瓶颈，而注意力能有效映射到并行硬件；（3）TBF压缩减少2.3倍参数和1.6倍延迟，仅产生0.007的BLEU-1变化成本，但过度压缩会静默丢弃聚合指标无法察觉的变化相关细节。

Conclusion: 融合模块的设计选择对检索性能与成本有显著影响，并非越复杂的模型越好。训练免的两阶段搜索策略是实际部署中的高效选择；Mamba在处理视觉Transformer典型patch数量时无实际速度优势；表示压缩需谨慎权衡，因为聚合指标可能掩盖对变化细节的损失。该研究为地球观测变化检索系统的融合模块设计提供了统计上可靠的设计指南。

Abstract: Operational Earth observation increasingly calls for answering queries such as ``find the image pairs where a new building appeared.'' This means searching an archive of before-and-after (bi-temporal) satellite image pairs and ranking each pair by how well it matches a natural-language description of the change. The component that performs this match, the fusion module that combines the ``before'' and ``after'' views, must be run at query time across many candidate pairs, so its speed largely sets the cost of every search. We present a controlled comparison of how to build that module. Using one fixed image encoder (a frozen CLIP model) and one training recipe for all variants, we evaluate eight designs drawn from three families: attention, state-space models (Mamba), and learned compression (our Temporal Bottleneck Fusion, TBF). Each design is tested on two benchmarks (LEVIR-CC and Dubai-CC) with ten random seeds, so the reported differences are statistically grounded. We outline three findings: first, a training-free two-stage search (a cheap difference model that shortlists candidates, followed by attention fusion that re-ranks them) matches or exceeds full-fusion recall on LEVIR-CC while cutting query cost $10$-$15\times$, with comparable R@1/R@5 on Dubai-CC; second, the linear-time scan of Mamba, attractive on paper, gives no speed benefit at the patch counts typical of vision transformers ($L{=}196$): the scan is limited by memory bandwidth, whereas attention maps cleanly onto parallel hardware; and third, compressing the fused representation (TBF) reduces parameters by $2.3\times$ and latency by $1.6\times$ for a change-only BLEU-1 cost of $0.007$, although more aggressive compression quietly discards change-relevant detail that aggregate metrics fail to reveal.

</details>


### [101] [ROAD: Reciprocal-Objective Alignment of Discriminative Semantics for 3D Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28581)
*Xiao Luo,Mingyang Du,Xin Zhou,Tianrui Feng,Xiwu Chen,Xiaofan Li,Jiangning Zhang,Dingkang Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ROAD框架，通过将判别式3D基础模型中的丰富先验知识迁移到扩散Transformer中，大幅降低3D生成模型的训练成本。仅使用工业基线Step1X-3D的1.5%训练数据即可达到高度竞争性的生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高质量3D生成主要依赖扩大模型容量和数据规模，计算成本高昂，且通常需要从零学习几何结构，忽视了判别式3D基础模型中已蕴含的丰富语义和结构先验。作者认为利用这些判别模型对3D世界的深刻理解可以显著降低生成成本。

Method: 提出ROAD框架，核心是互惠目标对齐策略（reciprocal-objective alignment），包含两个组件：(1) 全局语义凝聚（Holistic Semantic Condensing）用于强制全局语义一致性；(2) 结构最优对齐（Structural Optimal Alignment），将其建模为二分图匹配问题，用于严格对齐不同潜在空间间的微观几何细节。判别式3D基础模型仅在训练期间用于对齐监督，推理时不使用。

Result: 与工业基线Step1X-3D相比，ROAD仅使用其1.5%的训练数据即达到高度竞争的生成性能，显著降低了训练成本和高保真3D生成的计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过将判别式3D基础模型的先验知识高效迁移到扩散Transformer中，ROAD验证了利用判别模型3D理解力可大幅减少生成模型训练成本的可行性，为高保真3D生成提供了一条成本效益显著的替代路径。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D generation predominantly relies on scaling model capacity and data, which incurs prohibitive computational costs. This paradigm typically requires learning geometry from scratch and overlooks the rich semantic and structural priors already encapsulated in discriminative 3D foundation models. We contend that leveraging the profound understanding of the 3D world possessed by these discriminative models can significantly reduce generative cost. To this end, we propose ROAD, a framework that reduces the training cost of 3D generation by transferring these rich discriminative priors into diffusion transformers. To address the inherent semantic-structural heterogeneity between generative and discriminative latents, we introduce a reciprocal-objective alignment strategy. This method synergizes Holistic Semantic Condensing to enforce global semantic coherence and Structural Optimal Alignment, which is formulated as a bipartite matching problem to rigorously align microscopic geometric details between disparate latent spaces. The 3D foundation model is only used for training-time supervision of alignment and is not used at inference, incurring no additional inference cost. Compared with the industrial baseline Step1X-3D, the proposed ROAD achieves highly competitive generation performance with only 1.5% of the training data and significantly reduces training costs, effectively reducing the computational overhead of high-fidelity 3D generation. Code is available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/ROAD.

</details>


### [102] [MixFrag: Fragility-Guided Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28589)
*Md. Mehrab Hossain Opi,Robiul Islam Ryad,Md. Umar Faruk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixFrag是一个基于脆弱性引导的混合精度训练后量化(PTQ)框架，通过KL散度评估组件级量化脆弱性，并将比特分配建模为多选背包问题(MCKP)，在目标比特预算下实现Vision Transformer的分层自适应精度分配。在ImageNet-1K分类、COCO检测和实例分割任务上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ViT的PTQ方法通常对所有Transformer组件使用统一的比特宽度，忽略了不同组件对量化的异构敏感性，导致精度分配不高效。

Method: 1) 使用KL散度量化组件对量化的脆弱性（比较全精度与隔离量化输出分布）；2) 将比特分配问题建模为多选背包问题(MCKP)，在目标比特预算约束下进行自适应的逐层精度分配。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上多个ViT架构中取得有竞争力的分类性能；在COCO检测和实例分割上达到混合精度PTQ方法的SOTA，在MP3/MP3设置下比此前最优方法提升高达9.6 AP。

Conclusion: MixFrag被验证为有效的ViT混合精度训练后量化框架，其脆弱性度量与学习到的比特分配具有强相关性，为ViT在资源受限设备上的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) has emerged as an effective solution for deploying Vision Transformers (ViTs) on resource-constrained devices. However, existing PTQ methods typically employ uniform bit-widths across transformer components, overlooking their heterogeneous sensitivity to quantization and leading to inefficient precision allocation. In this paper, we propose {MixFrag, a fragility-guided mixed-precision PTQ framework for Vision Transformers. MixFrag first estimates component-level quantization fragility by measuring the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between full-precision and isolated quantized output distributions using a small calibration set. It then formulates bit allocation as a Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem (MCKP), enabling adaptive layer-wise precision assignment under a target bit budget. Extensive experiments on ImageNet-1K across multiple Vision Transformer architectures demonstrate that MixFrag achieves competitive classification performance under practical mixed-precision settings. Furthermore, evaluations on COCO object detection and instance segmentation show that MixFrag achieves state-of-the-art performance among existing mixed-precision PTQ methods, improving the previous best method by up to 9.6 AP under the challenging MP3/MP3 setting. Additional analyses validate the proposed fragility metric and demonstrate its strong correlation with the learned bit allocation. These results establish MixFrag as an effective framework for mixed-precision post-training quantization of Vision Transformers.

</details>


### [103] [Beacon: Knowing When and How to Perform Agentic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28595)
*Qixun Wang,Yang Shi,Letian Cheng,Zhuoran Zhang,Yan He,Yuqi Tang,Qi Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Ruizhe Chen,Tianrun Xu,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Haotian Wang,Xianghua Ying*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Beacon，一个智能体视觉推理模型，通过重新审视工具使用的两个维度（模式自适应性MA和工具效应TE），并引入必要性感知自适应奖励和提示引导能力扩展机制，在强化学习阶段同时提升自适应工具调用和困难问题上的工具使用能力，从而在基准测试中取得更优性能和更强的模式自适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体视觉推理模型面临两大问题：一是模式自适应性不足，即模型无法准确判断何时需要调用工具，导致不必要的计算开销；二是工具效应不佳，即工具在困难问题上带来的增益在很大程度上被简单问题上引入的错误所抵消。作者希望解决这两个核心缺陷，而非简单地赋予模型复杂的推理范式。

Method: 提出Beacon模型，核心包含两个机制：(1) Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward（必要性感知自适应奖励），根据任务实际需求鼓励模型自适应地进行工具调用，避免不必要的工具使用；(2) Hint-Guided Capability Expansion（提示引导能力扩展），在强化学习阶段增强模型在最具挑战性问题上的工具使用能力。同时定义了MA（模式自适应性）和TE（工具效应）两个量化维度用于系统分析。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上的大量实验表明，Beacon取得了强力的整体性能，相较现有智能体视觉推理模型在模式自适应性（MA）和工具效应（TE）两方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦模式自适应性和工具效应这两个关键维度，Beacon证明了智能体视觉推理的核心目标应当是提升复杂任务的成功率，而非仅仅引入复杂但低效的推理范式。必要性感知自适应奖励和提示引导能力扩展机制有效实现了自适应工具调用和真实工具性能增益。

Abstract: The fundamental goal of agentic visual reasoning is to improve the success rate of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on complex tasks, rather than merely equipping them with a sophisticated yet inefficient reasoning paradigm. In this work, we rethink agentic visual reasoning through two key dimensions of tool use: Mode Adaptiveness (MA) and Tool Effect (TE). Mode Adaptiveness characterizes whether an MLLM can recognize when tools are truly necessary and invoke them accordingly, thereby avoiding unnecessary computational overhead while improving performance on challenging problems that require tool assistance. Tool Effect characterizes the actual impact of tool use: tools should extend the model's capabilities on problems unsolvable through text-only reasoning, while avoiding additional errors on problems that the model can already solve without tools. We conduct a comprehensive analysis to quantify these two properties and empirically reveal that existing agentic visual reasoning models exhibit limited Mode Adaptiveness, while the gains produced by tool use on hard examples are largely offset by the harm introduced on easy examples that the models can already solve. Motivated by these observations, we propose Beacon, a novel agentic visual reasoning model that achieves stronger overall performance, improved Mode Adaptiveness, and genuine tool-induced performance gains. At the core of Beacon are the Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward and the Hint-Guided Capability Expansion mechanism in the reinforcement learning stage, which respectively encourage adaptive tool invocation based on task necessity and strengthen the model's tool-use capability on the most challenging problems. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate the strong overall performance of Beacon and its substantial improvements in both Mode Adaptiveness and Tool Effect.

</details>


### [104] [Chimera: Designing and Chinchilla-Scaling Hybrid Visual Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28611)
*Chongjian Ge,Hanwen Jiang,Tianyu Wang,Jiuxiang Gu,Yiran Xu,Ziwen Chen,Shaoteng Liu,Jing Shi,Yicong Hong,Zefan Cai,Hailin Jin,Hao Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Chimera，一种混合视觉扩散骨干架构，通过KDA注意力、MLA和多模态短卷积的组合实现O(N)复杂度的长上下文建模，并引入HeteroP模块级缩放方案拟合Chinchilla式计算最优定律，最终训练了11B参数（2B激活）的模型，在扩散损失计算效率上比Wan-2.1基线提升7.3倍，并能零样本从5秒视频外推到30秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成任务日益需要高分辨率图像、长视频和多模态上下文，但全注意力的二次方计算成本使其不可行，因此需要设计高效的混合注意力架构并建立可扩展的缩放方法论。

Method: Chimera采用无位置嵌入的栅格序流处理文本、图像和视频token，交替使用Kimi Delta Attention（KDA，O(N)复杂度）进行长上下文状态追踪、Multi-head Latent Attention（MLA）进行全局交互、模态感知短卷积捕获局部时空上下文，并用稀疏MoE层扩展容量。缩放方面提出HeteroP，按各张量的功能扇入和模型深度跨宽度和深度迁移超参数，构建一致调优的模型族来拟合Chinchilla式计算最优定律（激活参数、训练token数和图文视频数据比）。

Result: 三方面实验结果：(1) 稠密骨干比同等规模的Wan-2.1全注意力基线在扩散损失上计算效率高1.7倍，完整系统达7.3倍；(2) 无需长度特定微调，可从5秒训练片段零样本外推至30秒视频，最后5秒仅有6.5% FID退化；(3) 拟合定律表明图像预训练的计算最优在激活模型大小和训练token数之间近乎平分计算预算，而视频预训练在高预算下略微偏向模型大小。

Conclusion: Chimera与HeteroP为设计和扩展高效长上下文扩散架构建立了基础，证明了混合注意力骨干配合模块级缩放方案可以在保持高质量的同时大幅提升计算效率。

Abstract: Visual generation increasingly requires high-resolution images, long videos, and multimodal context, making the quadratic cost of full attention prohibitive. We introduce Chimera, a hybrid visual diffusion backbone with a principled scaling recipe. Chimera processes text, image, and video tokens in one raster-ordered stream without positional embeddings. It combines Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) for long-context state tracking with O(N) complexity, interleaved Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) for direct global interaction, and modality-aware short convolutions for local spatiotemporal context. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers expand capacity while controlling activated compute. To scale this heterogeneous architecture, we introduce HeteroP, a module-wise scheme that transfers hyperparameters across width and depth according to each tensor's functional fan-in and model depth. HeteroP yields a consistently tuned family used to fit Chinchilla-style compute-optimal laws for activated model size, training-token count, and image-video data ratio. Guided by these laws, we train an 11B-parameter Chimera with 2B activated parameters. Experiments show three results. First, measured by pretraining diffusion loss, the dense backbone is 1.7x as compute-efficient as a matched full-attention Wan-2.1 2B baseline, while the complete system reaches 7.3x. Second, without length-specific fine-tuning, Chimera extrapolates zero-shot from 5-second training clips to 30-second videos, with only 6.5% FID degradation in the last five seconds. Third, the fitted laws show that compute-optimal image pretraining divides compute nearly evenly between activated model size and training-token count, whereas video pretraining modestly favors model size at higher budgets. These results establish a foundation for designing and scaling efficient long-context diffusion architectures.

</details>


### [105] [PhiZero: A World Model Built Around Physical Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28624)
*Shuyao Shang,Yuqi Wang,Ruopeng Gao,Xu Chen,Tieniu Tan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhiZero，一种基于"物理语言"（一种紧凑的离散世界状态转换表示）构建的物理世界模型。它采用"先推理后渲染"（reason-then-render）的范式：首先以物理语言序列推理未来的世界演化，再将其渲染为视频。实验验证了其在生成与理解基准上的物理一致性建模能力，并展示了其在交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件模拟和零样本运动迁移方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理世界模型通常在像素空间中直接预测未来视频，将底层世界动态隐含在高维视觉预测器中，缺乏显式的物理推理能力。而人类能够从视觉经验中抽象出预测性结构，并以自然语言组织这些结构进行明确推理。受此启发，作者希望学习一种紧凑离散的"物理语言"表示，使物理世界模型能够显式地推理世界如何演化。

Method: PhiZero采用两阶段框架：(1) 通过自监督方式从真实世界视频中学习"物理语言"，这是一种紧凑的离散表示，用于编码世界状态的转变；(2) 采用"先推理后渲染"（reason-then-render）范式，即先用物理语言序列显式推理未来世界演化，再将该推理结果渲染成视频。

Result: 在生成和理解基准上的大量实验验证了PhiZero能够建模物理一致的世界演化能力，并进一步展示了其在真实交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件模拟以及零样本运动迁移等方面的优势。

Conclusion: PhiZero通过引入显式的物理语言中间表示，将物理世界模型的推理过程从隐式的像素层面前移到显式的离散语义层面，实现了更物理一致的世界演化建模，并为交互式建模和运动迁移等下游任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce PhiZero, a physical world model built around physical language, a compact discrete representation of world-state transitions. Existing physical world models typically predict future videos directly in pixel space, leaving the underlying world dynamics implicit within high-dimensional visual predictors. Motivated by humans' ability to abstract predictive structure from visual experience and organize it in natural language for explicit reasoning, we learn physical language from in-the-wild videos through self-supervision and use it to explicitly reason about how the physical world evolves. Accordingly, PhiZero adopts a reason-then-render paradigm: it first infers future world evolution as a physical-language sequence and then renders the inferred transitions into videos. Extensive experiments across generation and understanding benchmarks validate the ability of PhiZero to model physically coherent world evolution. We further show its potential for realistic and interactive world modeling, fine-grained action-conditioned simulation, and zero-shot motion transfer.

</details>


### [106] [ACE-Data-0: Human-Centric Ambient Capture as Embodied Data Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28625)
*Yukang Cao,Haozhe Xie,Beichen Wen,Runmao Yao,Yinghao Liu,Yue Huang,Zhichao Liao,Yunxiang Wang,Haiheng Liu,Xingshun Tian,Dawei Su,Long Zhuo,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang,Liang Pan,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人体中心的数据引擎ACE（Ambient Capture Engine），将真实家庭环境改造为空间校准、时间同步的录音棚式数据采集系统，并发布了包含150小时、1700万帧视频、75000次交互片段的多模态数据集ACE-Data-0，以及一个从信号到场景组件再到交互的分层基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能面临根本性的数据瓶颈：现有数据集在不同视角、模态或空间尺度上割裂了第一人称感知、全身运动、灵巧操作、物体状态、声音和触觉等信号，导致完整的感知-行动环路只能被部分观测，缺乏统一的多模态同步数据来支持具身智能的研究。

Method: ACE采用双尺度配置：桌面级配置解析手-物体交互，房间级配置捕捉全身运动、移动和家居环境交互；同步记录第一人称和多视角外置视频、全身和手部运动、物体几何与6-DoF轨迹、音频和触觉信号。数据采集采用目标级而非逐步指令，保留自然行为变异性。同时引入从信号到场景组件再到交互的分层基准。

Result: ACE-Data-0包含150小时和1700万帧视频，覆盖200个任务类别，50名参与者在2个环境中执行共75000个交互片段；涵盖原子操作、长时家务活动链和人-场景交互。对最先进方法的评估揭示了在接触、遮挡、自我运动及长时间跨度下的显著性能差距。

Conclusion: ACE-Data-0提供了感知、运动学和接触监督对齐的同步人体演示数据，为模仿学习、世界模型、视觉-语言-动作系统和具身AI提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence faces a fundamental data bottleneck. Models must capture how first-person perception, whole-body motion, dexterous manipulation, object state, sound, and touch evolve together as humans pursue goals over time. Existing datasets fragment this experience across viewpoints, modalities, or spatial scales, leaving the full perception-action loop only partially observed. We introduce the Ambient Capture Engine (ACE), a human-centric data engine that transforms real home environments into spatially calibrated, temporally synchronized recording studios. ACE operates at two complementary scales: a table-scale configuration resolves hand-object manipulation, while a room-scale configuration captures whole-body motion, locomotion, and interactions across a furnished home. ACE records egocentric and multi-view exocentric video, full-body and articulated hand motion, object geometry and 6-DoF trajectories, audio, and tactile signals as a unified multisensory stream. Using ACE, we build ACE-Data-0, comprising 150 hours and 17M video frames across 200 task categories, performed by 50 participants in 2 environments, for a total of 75,000 interaction episodes. The dataset spans atomic manipulation, long-horizon chains of household activities, and human-scene interaction, while preserving natural behavioral variation through goal-level rather than step-by-step instructions. We further introduce a hierarchical benchmark that progresses from signals to scene components and then to interactions. Evaluations of state-of-the-art methods expose substantial gaps under contact, occlusion, egomotion, and long temporal horizons. ACE-Data-0 provides synchronized human demonstrations with aligned perceptual, kinematic, and contact supervision, offering a scalable foundation for imitation learning, world models, vision-language-action systems, and embodied AI.

</details>


### [107] [ReToken: One Token to Improve Vision-Language Models for Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28627)
*Yao Xiao,Reuben Tan,Zhen Zhu,Yuqun Wu,Jianfeng Gao,Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReToken是一个可学习的检索目标嵌入，能从预填充的视觉KV缓存中挑选出与查询相关的稀疏视觉token，在不重训模型的条件下缓解长视觉上下文中视觉-语言模型性能退化与GPU显存瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视觉上下文是视觉-语言模型面临的挑战：随着干扰物（distractor）数量增加，模型性能显著退化；同时直接处理全部token在GPU显存约束下计算不可行。

Method: 提出ReToken，即一个单一的可学习嵌入，将其训练为显式的检索目标，用于从预填充的视觉KV缓存中选出与查询相关的稀疏视觉token集合，从而降低后续处理的token量。

Result: 仅在小规模图像QA数据集上训练，ReToken在图像与视频基准上均取得稳定提升：Visual Haystacks上使Qwen3VL-8B提升13.4点、InternVL3.5提升12.4点（相对提升>20%）；LVBench上零样本迁移到长视频任务，对Qwen3VL-8B获得8.0点增益。由于设计轻量，训练和长视频推理均可单张H100完成。

Conclusion: ReToken通过轻量的可学习检索嵌入有效解决长视觉上下文下的性能退化与显存瓶颈，且泛化性良好，可零样本迁移到长视频场景，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long visual context poses a challenge for vision-language models: performance degrades as the number of distractors grows, and processing all tokens at once is computationally infeasible under GPU memory constraints. We present ReToken, a single learnable embedding trained as an explicit retrieval target that selects a sparse set of query-relevant visual tokens from a pre-filled visual KV cache. Trained on only a small image-QA dataset, ReToken yields consistent gains across image and video benchmarks: on Visual Haystacks it improves Qwen3VL-8B by 13.4 points and InternVL3.5 by 12.4 points (>20% relative), and on LVBench it transfers zero-shot to long video for an 8.0-point gain with Qwen3VL-8B. Thanks to its lightweight design, both training and long-video inference fit on a single H100. Code is available at: https://github.com/avaxiao/ReToken

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Prompt Chaining in Practice: A Case Study in Automated Scholarly Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27210)
*Andrei Lazarev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种多阶段提示链（prompt chaining）方法，用于自动生成学术文献摘要，并实现了AI SciBrief系统。实验表明，该方法在可靠性（100%成功率 vs 基线50%失败率）和质量（ROUGE-L F1 0.507 vs 0.486）上均显著优于优化的单次提示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术出版物指数级增长，需要自动化工具进行有效信息综合。然而，简单的单次提示方法在复杂综合任务中缺乏可靠性和质量保障，因此需要更稳健的架构模式。

Method: 提出多阶段提示链（multi-stage prompt chaining）方法，并实现于AI SciBrief系统中。通过将复杂生成任务分解为多个连续的处理阶段，每个阶段由独立的提示驱动，并与经过精心优化的单次提示基线进行对比实验，以人类撰写的"金标准"报告为参照评估性能。

Result: 提示链方法在可靠性上达到100%成功率，而优化的单次提示基线在50%的运行中失败；在质量上，提示链方法取得更高的ROUGE-L F1分数（0.507 vs 0.486），主要得益于更高的精确率。

Conclusion: 提示链是一种更可靠、更有效的工程方法，适用于复杂的多步骤生成任务，能显著降低整体式提示中固有的失败和不一致性风险。

Abstract: The exponential growth of scholarly publications requires automated tools for effective information synthesis. However, simple, single-shot prompting methods often lack the reliability and quality required for complex synthesis tasks. This paper introduces and empirically evaluates a multi-stage prompt chaining methodology as a more reliable architectural pattern for such tasks. This approach is implemented in our system, AI SciBrief, which automatically generates scholarly digests. We conducted a comparative experiment, measuring the performance of our prompt chaining method against a carefully optimized single-shot baseline. Both systems were evaluated against a human-authored "gold standard" report for the "Education" domain. The results demonstrate a significant difference in reliability: our prompt chaining method achieved a 100% success rate, whereas the optimized baseline failed in 50% of its runs. In terms of quality, the proposed method also demonstrated a clear advantage, achieving a superior ROUGE-L F1-score (0.507 vs. 0.486), driven primarily by higher precision. We conclude that prompt chaining is a more dependable and effective engineering approach for complex, multi-step generative tasks, significantly mitigating the risks of failure and inconsistency inherent in monolithic prompts.

</details>


### [109] [AI-assisted pre-review of open-source software submissions: an experience report from BOSC 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27228)
*Tazro Ohta,Nomi L. Harris,Seth Carbon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BOSC 2026会议上使用生成式AI进行论文摘要预评审的实验。作者开发了bosc-pre-review智能技能和Runabilly工具，对六个评审标准进行自动评估，其中Runabilly在隔离的Docker容器中构建和测试项目以确保安全性。AI仅收集证据供评审员参考，所有接收决策仍由人类做出。调查显示大多数评审员认为预评审有用，但倾向于交叉验证AI结论而非盲目接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI使得提交论文材料变得更加容易，会议面临稿件数量激增的挑战，志愿评审员负担过重。作者希望探索生成式AI能否通过预评审摘要来减轻人力负担。

Method: 作者基于BOSC已有的详细评审细则（涵盖开放性、开源许可证、可运行性等标准），构建了bosc-pre-review智能技能评估六个评审标准，并开发了Runabilly工具在一次性Docker容器中安全地构建和测试每个项目。AI仅收集证据供人类评审员参考，所有最终决策仍由人类做出。评审期后通过问卷调查评审员对预评审有用性的反馈。

Result: 大多数参与调查的评审员表示预评审工具是有用的，但他们倾向于将AI的结论与自己的判断进行交叉验证，而不是无条件接受AI的结果。

Conclusion: 生成式AI预评审在减轻评审负担方面具有潜力，但人类评审员更认可AI作为辅助工具而非决策者的角色，人机协作的评审模式值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Most conferences rely on peer-review of submissions, but as generative AI makes it easier than ever to prepare submission materials, some conferences are seeing an overwhelming surge of submissions. We wanted to see if generative AI could help our conference's volunteer reviewers by pre-reviewing abstracts for certain criteria. The Bioinformatics Open Source Conference (BOSC) was well-positioned to experiment with this, as we already had a detailed rubric used by reviewers to evaluate submitted abstracts on multiple criteria, including openness (public availability of the code or other content associated with the project), valid open source license, and "runnability" (how easy it is to download, build, and run the project - an important measure of reusability). For BOSC 2026, we built bosc-pre-review, an agentic skill that assessed six review criteria, and Runabilly, which builds and tests each project in a disposable Docker container for safety. The AI only gathered evidence to present to the reviewers; humans made every decision regarding the acceptance of the abstracts. After the review period, we surveyed the reviewers to determine how useful they found the pre-review. Most of those who responded said they found it useful, but they preferred to check the AI's conclusions against their own, rather than accepting the AI results unquestioningly.

</details>


### [110] [Sympathetic Framing: Evaluating AI Alignment across Sociodemographic Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27232)
*Haran Shani-Narkiss,Michael Fire,Oren Tsur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过大规模实证评估（3011名英国成人、7个LLM），考察大语言模型是否能在政治冲突新闻报道框架中与人类情感感知对齐，发现模型间对齐程度差异巨大（相关系数0.4至0.789），且即便高性能模型在不同人口统计子群体间也存在统计显著的细微对齐差异，揭示"差分对齐"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益影响信息消费和世界观形成，传统研究关注AI偏见，但忽视了LLM是否理解通过文本框架传达的情感细微差别（如对冲突一方的同情）。需要一个大规模、人口多样化的实证研究来系统评估LLM与人类情感感知的对齐程度。

Method: 采用YouGov调查，招募3011名具有代表性的英国成人样本，让其对涉及政治和地缘政治冲突的新闻标题判断是否唤起对指定冲突方的同情；同时让七个LLM（包括GPT-5.2、Mistral Large 2512等）回答相同问题；计算AI与人类评估之间的相关性，并按年龄、性别、教育水平、地缘政治知识和冲突先验倾向等人口统计子群体进行分层分析。

Result: AI与人类评估的相关系数跨度大：最高为GPT-5.2（0.789），最低为Mistral Large 2512（0.4）。领先模型在所有人口统计子群体中总体与人类判断广泛一致，但各群体之间存在统计显著差异，表明对齐并非普遍均质。

Conclusion: 这是迄今为止对LLM理解新闻框架最全面的评估。核心发现是"差分对齐"问题：即使聚合层面性能高，AI对齐也并非普遍——它可能与人口统计特征和文化规范存在不同的对应关系。开发和评估伦理且有用的AI系统时，必须考虑或正视这一差分对齐需求。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly shaping how we consume information and form our worldview. This raises concerns beyond bias in AI: do LLMs grasp the emotional nuances conveyed via textual framing? In this work, we empirically evaluate how well an array of LLMs aligns with human emotional perception. Considering news headlines covering political and geopolitical conflicts, both human participants (n = 3011, a representative sample of the U.K. adult population, via a YouGov survey) and seven LLMs answered whether headlines evoked sympathy for a specified side in a conflict. We find that the correlation between AI and human evaluations varies across models, ranging from very high (0.789, GPT-5.2) to medium (0.4 ,Mistral Large 2512). Crucially, the leading models are broadly aligned with human judgments across all demographic subgroups, including age, gender, level of education, prior geopolitical knowledge, and participants' predispositions regarding the conflict, although there are statistically significant differences between groups. This research, with its robust design and large, demographically diverse dataset, offers the most comprehensive evaluation of LLMs' comprehension of news framing to date. Findings highlight an important, often-ignored aspect of differential alignment: even when aggregate performance is high, AI alignment is not universal -- it may correspond differently with demographic features and cultural norms. Considering or ignoring the need for differential alignment may therefore have significant implications for the development of ethical and useful AI systems.

</details>


### [111] [LayerRAG-Bench: A Cross-Layer Reliability Benchmark for Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27353)
*Musa Shams*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LayerRAG-Bench，一个跨层可靠性的受控基准测试，涵盖8个企业领域、240个任务、9个故障场景、2种契约模式和38880条实时任务级记录，评估9个来自OpenAI、Anthropic和Gemini的模型。研究表明模式归一化可将模式漂移成功率从0.000提升到0.913，但无法修复过期证据、缺失工具输出、权限拒绝和错误会话上下文等问题。基于接地性的评估在过期和错误会话证据下产生大量假阳性，支持分层评估原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式RAG系统可能在输出看似有依据的答案时，在证据层、工具契约层、授权层或会话状态层失败。现有评估方法无法区分这些不同层面的可靠性问题，需要一种受控的跨层可靠性基准来系统性地识别和评估各层故障。

Method: 构建LayerRAG-Bench基准测试，覆盖8个企业领域、240个任务、9个故障场景、2种契约模式，并在9个来自OpenAI、Anthropic和Gemini的模型上进行38,880条实时任务级记录评估。通过模式归一化干预和接地性评估分别检验不同层次故障的修复效果。

Result: 模式归一化将模式漂移成功率从0.000提升至0.913，但对过期证据、缺失工具输出、权限拒绝和错误会话上下文等故障无效。仅基于接地性的评估在过期证据和错误会话证据场景下产生大量假阳性结果。

Conclusion: 可靠性干预措施应针对其目标层进行评估和归功，不应被视为通用的修复方案。文章支持分层评估原则：可靠性干预应被认可修复其目标层，但不应误认为对所有层的普遍解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic retrieval-augmented generation systems can produce answers that appear grounded while failing at the evidence, tool-contract, authorization, or session-state layer. We introduce LayerRAG-Bench, a controlled cross-layer reliability benchmark with 8 enterprise domains, 240 tasks, 9 fault scenarios, 2 contract modes, and 38,880 live task-level records across nine models from OpenAI, Anthropic, and Gemini. Schema normalization raises schema-drift success from 0.000 to 0.913, but stale evidence, missing tool output, denied permissions, and wrong-session context are not recovered by schema normalization. Groundedness-only evaluation also produces substantial false positives under stale and wrong-session evidence. These results support a layer-specific evaluation principle: a reliability intervention should be credited for repairing its target layer without being mistaken for a universal fix.

</details>


### [112] [BridgeAlign: Bridging Preference Alignment for Humanities and Social Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27366)
*Ru Peng,Haokai Xu,Xijun Gu,Tianyu Zhao,Zhiting Fan,Yawen Zeng,Yihong Zhuang,Jinyang Zhang,Kexin Yang,Jian Wu,Hao Chen,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Junbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BridgeAlign是首个面向广泛人文社会科学（HSS）领域的偏好对齐流水线，通过三阶段（种子策展、偏好数据合成、偏好优化）生成超过21万条合成偏好样本，使Qwen3-8B在17个基准上取得最优平均表现，且同时在人类偏好能力和知识能力上领先而无权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM数据合成主要针对有可验证答案的领域，忽视了开放式的文社科学科，而文社科的答案质量依赖于细微的主观判断而非客观正确性。虽然偏好对齐天然适合此类任务，但现有方法要么成本高昂，要么不专门针对广泛的文社科学科设计。

Method: 三阶段流水线：1) 种子策展——通过启发式/LLM过滤和文本精炼从网络语料中筛选HSS种子文档；2) 偏好数据合成——基于角色化的指令反转与问答一致性检查生成偏好三元组；3) 偏好优化——超越简单的"人类vs模型"启发式，先将偏好锚定在HSS质量评分标准上，再通过受控质量退化生成过渡响应，形成近边界偏好对以实现更细粒度的质量判别。最终对齐超过21万条合成偏好样本。

Result: BridgeAlign使Qwen3-8B在17个基准上对11个强基线取得最优平均表现；更重要的是，同时在人类偏好能力和基于知识的任务上领先，二者之间无trade-off，实验结果和现有理论均支持这一结论。

Conclusion: BridgeAlign证明了通过专门设计的偏好对齐流水线，可以在广泛的文社科学科中实现高质量的能力提升，且人类偏好与知识能力可以兼得而非互斥，为开放领域LLM对齐提供了新的范式。

Abstract: While data synthesis for large language models (LLMs) is prevalent, it primarily targets domains with verifiable answers, overlooking open-ended humanities and social sciences (HSS), where nuanced quality judgments matter more than objective correctness. This makes preference alignment a natural paradigm for broad HSS tasks. Yet existing methods are either costly or not tailored to broad HSS disciplines. We thus propose BridgeAlign, among the first preference-alignment pipelines for broad HSS disciplines, with three phases: i) Seed Curation: curating HSS seed documents from web corpora via heuristic/LLM-based filtering and text refinement; ii) Preference Data Synthesis: generating preference triplets via persona-based instruction inversion with Q&A consistency checks; iii) Preference Optimization: moving beyond naive human-vs-model heuristics by first grounding preferences in HSS quality rubric, then generating transitional responses via controlled quality degradation to form near-boundary preference pairs for finer-grained quality discrimination. Aligning over 210k synthetic preference samples, BridgeAlign enables Qwen3-8B to achieve the best average across 17 benchmarks against 11 strong baselines; importantly, leading on both human-preference and knowledge-based capabilities at once, with no trade-off between them, as supported by extensive experiments and contextualized by existing theories.

</details>


### [113] [HSS-Synth: Humanities and Social Sciences Data Synthesis for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27379)
*Ru Peng,Tianyu Zhao,Xijun Gu,Zhiting Fan,Haokai Xu,Jinyang Zhang,Yawen Zeng,Yihong Zhuang,Kexin Yang,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Junbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HSS-Synth，这是首个针对人文社会科学（HSS）领域的数据合成pipeline，采用以学科为中心的范式，覆盖14个主流领域，通过种子文档构建、"需求+人设"反向翻译和教师强制的答问生成三个步骤，产出了237k高质量多样化的指令微调样本，在16个基准上超越14个领先基线，使Qwen3-8B-Base达到新SOTA并接近官方版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量、多样化的数据对大语言模型至关重要但稀缺且昂贵。数据合成是可行的替代方案，但现有工作聚焦于封闭式任务，忽视了人文社科领域开放性问题所带来的合成挑战；此前的研究是能力导向的、碎片化的尝试，缺乏系统的领域覆盖和统一的合成范式。

Method: HSS-Synth由三个步骤组成：(1) 通过多步过滤和文本精炼（由judge评估）从网页语料构建种子文档；(2) 通过指定"需求+人设"将种子文档反向翻译为多样化且忠实的指令，并执行严格的问答对齐检查；(3) 采用教师强制的答问生成方式，在响应生成期间将种子文档作为输入喂入，以锚定语义、减少幻觉并保留语气和完整性。

Result: HSS-Synth产出了237k高质量、多样化的指令微调样本，在16个基准上优于14个领先基线；微调后的Qwen3-8B-Base达到新的SOTA，接近官方Qwen3-8B，在人类偏好和知识能力上均有提升且无性能跷跷板效应；大量实验证明了其鲁棒性和可迁移性。

Conclusion: HSS-Synth首次系统性地解决了HSS领域的数据合成问题，验证了以学科为中心的合成范式在开放领域任务上的有效性，为该领域未来的数据合成研究提供了新的思路和可复现的开源工具。

Abstract: High-quality, diverse data are vital for large language models (LLMs) but remain scarce and costly. Data synthesis is a viable alternative and succeeds on closed tasks, yet the humanities and social sciences (HSS) are overlooked, and their open-ended nature makes synthesis challenging. Moving beyond prior capability-centric, fragmented attempts, we adopt a subject-centric paradigm, define the first HSS domain system covering 14 mainstream fields, and introduce HSS-Synth, the first data synthesis pipeline for HSS. HSS-Synth comprises: (1) constructing seed documents from web corpora via multi-step filtering and text refinement evaluated by a judge; (2) specifying "requirements + persona" to backtranslate seed documents into diverse yet faithful instructions with a strict Q&A alignment check; and (3) breaking LLM response limits via teacher-forced Answering that feeds seed documents during response generation to anchor semantics, reduce hallucinations, and preserve tone and integrity. HSS-Synth yields 237k high-quality, diverse instruction-tuning samples that outperform 14 leading baselines on 16 benchmarks. The fine-tuned Qwen3-8B-Base sets a new SOTA and approaches the official Qwen3-8B, improving both human preference and knowledge capabilities without performance seesaws. Extensive experiments demonstrate HSS-Synth's robustness and transferability. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/pengr/HSS-Synth.

</details>


### [114] [Same Facts, Different Diagnosis: Measuring and Mitigating Narrative Anchoring in Clinical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27384)
*Prabhjot Singh,Pritam Deka,Vijay Chennareddy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在临床诊断推理中对社会语言学语域敏感，而非仅关注临床内容，并将这种失败模式命名为"Narrative Anchoring"（叙事锚定）。作者构建了包含1,000个USMLE临床病例的基准数据集，每个病例以三种社会语言学上不同的"人设"重写，在独立审计的事实保留保证下进行。在跨越三个架构家族和规模的七个语言模型中，该偏差在直接提示条件下均具有统计显著性，Narrative Anchoring Gap为0.064至0.151。链式推理和显式去偏指令只能部分减少偏差，且常伴随准确性崩溃。作者提出NarrativeShield，一个三智能体管道，在诊断推理前结构化提取并验证临床事实，将Narrative Anchoring Gap降至接近零（-0.004至0.037），并在所有方法中达到最低的严重不稳定决策率，代价是大多数模型适度且机制上可预期的准确性损失。压力测试表明执行去偏干预受零样本指令跟随能力门控，而非仅提示内容本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的临床诊断推理中关于大语言模型偏差的研究主要集中在操纵显式身份标记（如种族、收入），而忽略了语域（register）这一独立于临床内容的偏差通道。作者旨在证明即使不包含任何人口统计标记，仅改变社会语言学语域即可导致诊断输出发散，揭示一种新的、更隐蔽的偏差模式。

Method: 1) 构建1,000个USMLE临床病例数据集，每个改写为三种社会语言学不同的人设，由独立模型审计事实保留；2) 在七个跨架构、跨规模的语言模型上测试直接提示下的偏差；3) 对比链式推理、显式去偏指令等现有缓解策略；4) 提出NarrativeShield三智能体管道——在诊断推理前结构化提取并验证临床事实；5) 使用非指令微调基础模型进行压力测试。

Result: 直接提示下所有模型均表现出统计显著的Narrative Anchoring偏差，Gap范围为0.064至0.151。链式推理和显式去偏指令仅部分减少偏差且常伴随准确性崩溃。NarrativeShield将偏差降至接近零（-0.004至0.037），达到最低的严重不稳定决策率（DSS<0.8），对大多数模型仅有适度且可预期的准确性损失。压力测试显示去偏干预的执行受零样本指令跟随能力门控。

Conclusion: Narrative Anchoring是一种独立于人口统计标记的、由社会语言学语域变化驱动的系统性临床诊断偏差。NarrativeShield通过在推理前结构化提取和验证临床事实，提供了一种有效的缓解方案。研究中还揭示了去偏干预的可执行性受模型指令跟随能力的根本性制约，并发布了经人工验证事实保留的数据集作为该领域的标准资源。

Abstract: Large language models used for clinical diagnostic reasoning are sensitive to sociolinguistic register, not just clinical content. We term this failure mode Narrative Anchoring: identical clinical facts expressed in different registers cause diagnostic outputs to diverge. Unlike prior demographic-bias work, which manipulates explicit identity tokens such as race or income, our benchmark isolates register as the sole channel of variation, with no demographic marker present in any form. We construct a dataset of 1,000 USMLE clinical vignettes, each rewritten into three sociolinguistically distinct personas under an independently audited fact-preservation guarantee, verified by a separate model that never sees the generation prompt. Across seven language models spanning three architecture families and scales, Narrative Anchoring is statistically significant under direct prompting in every model tested, with a Narrative Anchoring Gap of 0.064 to 0.151. Chain-of-thought reasoning and explicit debiasing instructions reduce the bias only partially, and their apparent gains are frequently confounded by accuracy collapse. We introduce NarrativeShield, a three-agent pipeline that structurally extracts and verifies clinical facts before diagnostic reasoning begins, reducing the Narrative Anchoring Gap to near-zero ($-0.004$ to $0.037$) and achieving the lowest rate of severely unstable decisions (DSS $<$ 0.8) of any method across all models, at a modest and mechanistically expected accuracy cost for most models. A stress test using a non-instruction-tuned base model shows that executing a debiasing intervention at all is gated by zero-shot instruction-following ability, not prompt content alone. We release our dataset, human-validated for fact preservation, as a standalone resource for studying register-based clinical bias.

</details>


### [115] [AHA-Memes: A Fine-Grained Multimodal Benchmark for Understanding Hate in Arabic Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27393)
*Mohamed Bayan Kmainasi,Ali Ezzat Shahroor,Abul Hasnat,Md. Rafiul Biswas,Wajdi Zaghouani,Firoj Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AHA-Memes，这是首个大规模阿拉伯语仇恨模因基准数据集，包含5K条人工标注的多标签数据（细粒度仇恨类型分类）及约66K条银标签数据，并对多种模型进行了系统基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨模因是日益增长的多模态在线危害形式，但阿拉伯语仇恨模因检测研究严重不足，现有阿拉伯语模因资源主要聚焦于宣传或粗粒度的有害内容标签，缺乏细粒度的多标签仇恨模因基准。

Method: 构建AHA-Memes数据集：包含5K条人工标注的多标签仇恨模因，采用捕捉仇恨类型（攻击策略）的分类体系；另提供约66K条银标签数据。基准测试覆盖仅文本、仅图像、晚期融合多模态模型，以及少样本上下文学习（ICL）和零样本/微调设置下的开放与封闭权重视觉语言模型（VLMs）。

Result: 实验结果建立了强基线，揭示了在文化背景丰富的阿拉伯语仇恨模因检测中面临的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 该数据集、标注指南和评估脚本均已公开以支持未来研究，有助于推动阿拉伯语仇恨模因检测领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Hateful memes are a growing form of multimodal online harm, where hostile intent is often conveyed through the joint interpretation of images, text, cultural references, and implicit targets. While hateful meme detection has advanced in high-resource languages, Arabic remains underexplored, with existing meme resources focusing mainly on propaganda or coarse harmful-content labels. We introduce AHA-Memes (Arabic HAteful Memes), which is, to our knowledge, the first large-scale Arabic hateful meme benchmark with fine-grained, multi-label annotations. The dataset includes 5K manually annotated memes using a taxonomy that captures hate types, i.e., attack strategies. We further provide ~66K silver-labeled memes to support future studies. We benchmark text-only, image-only, and late-fusion multimodal models, as well as few-shot in-context learning (ICL) and open- and closed-weight Vision-Language Models (VLMs) under zero-shot and fine-tuning settings. Our results establish strong baselines and highlight key challenges in culturally grounded Arabic hateful meme detection. We release the dataset, annotation guidelines, and evaluation scripts to support future research. WARNING: This paper contains examples that may be disturbing to readers.

</details>


### [116] [Benchmarking LLM Competence on Logical Inference over Probability Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27405)
*Nayera Hasan,Jack Greff,Alvin Grissom*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个关于概率算子推理的基准测试，包含14,320个程序生成的英文提示，覆盖15种推理模板，系统性地变化问题形式、否定策略和表面内容。评估了29个大语言模型后发现大部分模型存在与逻辑形式无关的答案偏差（系统性地偏好Yes或No），只有9个模型超过随机水平，并且在问题形式、动词短语、性别和名字来源等多个维度上都存在偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言中不确定性表达和推理无处不在，医学、法律等高风险领域都需要对不确定性表达进行有效推理。虽然大语言模型越来越多地被用于评估逻辑推理任务，但要区分原则性的符号推理与聪明的表面模式匹配十分困难，因此需要一个专门的基准来系统评估模型对层级认知情态词（如probably, might, must）的概率算子推理能力。

Method: 构建了一个包含14,320个程序化生成的英文提示的基准测试，覆盖15种推理模板，系统变化问题形式、否定策略和表面内容。评估了29个大语言模型，并引入了"能力下限"（competence floor）指标——即模型在Yes正确和No正确条目上准确率的较差值，用于衡量答案偏差。同时还在问题形式、动词短语/活动、名字的性别和来源等维度上进行偏差测试。

Result: 大多数模型表现出与逻辑形式无关的答案偏差，系统性地偏好Yes或No。只有9/29的模型超过了随机水平。此外，在问题形式、动词短语/活动、名字性别和来源等所有测试维度上均发现了偏差。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在概率算子推理任务上表现不佳，大多数模型存在严重的答案偏差而非真正的逻辑推理能力，这突显了开发更可靠的推理评估方法和改进模型概率推理能力的必要性。

Abstract: Both expressions of uncertainty and inferences are ubiquitous in natural language, and valid inferences over natural-language expressions of uncertainty are necessary for not only everyday conversations but also for high-stakes domains such as medicine and law. While large language models are increasingly evaluated on logical reasoning tasks, disentangling principled, symbolic reasoning from clever surface-level pattern matching is fraught with difficulty. We introduce a benchmark for reasoning over probability operators--inference over sentences with gradable epistemic modals (e.g., probably, might, must) containing 14,320 procedurally-generated English prompts across fifteen inference templates, systematically varying question form, negation strategy, and surface content. Evaluating 29 models, we find that most show answer biases independent of the logical form, a systematic preference for Yes or No. We summarize this with a competence floor: the worse of a model's accuracy on Yes-correct and No-correct items. Only 9 of 29 models exceed random chance. We also test variations in question form, verb phrases/activity, and both the gender and origin of names used in the prompts, finding biases across every axis.

</details>


### [117] [Selecting Open-Weight Language Models for Zero-Shot Intent Classification: A Systematic Evaluation of 41 Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27421)
*Parishruthi Ganesh,Gerry Dozier,Cheryl Seals*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对41个开源语言模型（15个家族，135M-9B参数范围）在8个英文单标签意图分类数据集上进行系统性零样本评估，并从准确率、置信度校准、鲁棒性、统计可靠性、部署效率和基准饱和六个维度提供选择开源模型的实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意图分类是任务型对话系统的核心组件，但实践者在计算资源、延迟和鲁棒性约束下缺乏系统性的开源模型选择指南。现有研究未能在统一框架下比较不同规模、不同指令微调策略的开源模型。

Method: 对41个开源权重语言模型（覆盖15个模型家族，参数范围为135M到9B）在8个英文单标签意图分类数据集上进行系统性零样本评估，另以ATIS作为五样本辅助结果。评估维度包括精确匹配准确率、置信度校准、对输入扰动的鲁棒性、模型排名的统计可靠性（成对McNemar检验）、部署效率和基准饱和度分析。数据集涵盖标准基准、大规模语音助手语料和生产环境电商数据。

Result: 指令微调的3B模型可超越多个评估的7B基础模型；MASSIVE上领先模型之间的差异在成对McNemar检验下统计上不可区分；SNIPS等广泛使用的基准已饱和，不再能有效区分当前开源模型的性能；指令微调对置信度校准的影响不一致而非普遍有害。

Conclusion: 研究为意图分类场景下开源语言模型的选择与评估提供了实践指导，指出3B指令微调模型是性价比高的选择，同时警示部分基准基准已饱和需要更新评测体系。

Abstract: Intent classification is a core component of task-oriented dialogue systems, yet practitioners have limited systematic guidance for selecting deployable open-weight language models under compute, latency, and robustness constraints. We present a systematic zero-shot evaluation of 41 open-weight language models spanning 15 families and the 135M--9B parameter range across eight English single-label intent-classification datasets. A ninth dataset, ATIS, uses five labeled demonstrations and is reported as an auxiliary five-shot result. The evaluation includes standard benchmarks, a large-scale voice-assistant corpus, and production-derived e-commerce datasets. Beyond exact-match accuracy, we analyze confidence calibration, robustness to realistic input perturbations, statistical reliability of model rankings, deployment efficiency, and benchmark saturation. Our results show that instruction-tuned 3B models can outperform several evaluated 7B base models, that differences among leading models on MASSIVE are statistically indistinguishable under pairwise McNemar tests, and that widely used benchmarks such as SNIPS have become saturated and no longer meaningfully discriminate among current open-weight models. Instruction tuning's effect on confidence calibration is inconsistent rather than uniformly harmful. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting and evaluating open-weight language models for intent classification.

</details>


### [118] [SkillSmith: Learning to Compose Parametric Skills and Textual Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27497)
*Lucio M. Dery,Benedict Aaron Tjandra,Siavash Samiei,Adhiguna Kuncoro,Zohar Yahav,Jiajun Shen,Arthur Szlam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSmith方法，通过将模型权重视为LLM可以原生推理的额外模态，实现文本知识与参数化技能（prefix权重）的统一整合，从而完成指令引导的参数合成并超越单模态（仅文本或仅权重）方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体系统通常将文本知识组织（通过组合与反思）和参数化技能库构建（通过权重空间合并）视为两条正交的研究路径，导致文本与模型权重的无缝集成以进行针对性性能提升的问题尚未被探索。

Method: 将模型权重视为额外模态，通过prefix-tuning实现参数化学习，并增强LLM使其能够同时接收prefix权重和丰富的文本数据（这些数据捕捉与目标能力的关系）。增强后的LLM（称为SkillSmith）根据指令引导进行参数化合成，直接输出体现目标技能的新的prefix权重。

Result: SkillSmith显著优于仅文本和仅权重空间的基线方法，实现了单模态（仅文本或仅权重）适应方法无法触及的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了文本与模型权重之间的模态鸿沟，证明将模型权重作为LLM可推理的额外模态，能够实现更优的跨模态参数合成，为智能体的技能积累与复用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Agentic systems driven by large language models (LLMs) regularly feature two key mechanisms to autonomously solve complex problems: synthesizing text-based knowledge and procedures from past experiences and building parametric (weight-space) skill libraries for recurring sub-goals. To date, research has largely treated these as orthogonal pursuits: either organizing textual knowledge through composition and reflection, or consolidating parametric skills via weight-space merging. Consequently, the seamless integration of text and model weights for targeted performance improvements remains largely unexplored. This work bridges this modality gap by treating model weights as an additional modality that an LLM can natively reason over. We instantiate parametric learning via prefix-tuning and augment an LLM to ingest both prefix weights and rich textual data which capture relationships to a target capability. Our augmented LLM, which we call SkillSmith, synthesizes these inputs to perform instruction-steered parametric synthesis, directly outputting new prefix weights that manifest the target skill. We demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms both text-only and weight-space-only baselines, unlocking performance gains that are out of reach for uni-modal (text-only or weight-only) adaptations.

</details>


### [119] [Models for minimalist RAG: B1ade 335M Embedding and 1B Parameter Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27506)
*Shreyas Subramanian,Mecit Gungor,Vikram Elango*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出B1ade，一种资源高效的RAG架构，由参数融合的紧凑嵌入模型（B1ade-embed，335M参数）和基于GRPO训练的专用SLM（B1ade-1B）组成。核心发现是"涌现式归因"：在没有显式引用监督的情况下，模型在42.4%的回复中自动引用检索段落，超出训练分布5.5个百分点，表明接地行为可在RL训练中作为精度最大化策略自然涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统假设语言模型和嵌入模型需要大规模预训练及显式接地监督，这造成高昂的计算成本。作者旨在证明通过策略性的模型组合和奖励设计，无需大规模预训练即可实现资源高效的RAG系统。

Method: B1ade由两部分组成：(1) B1ade-embed：335M参数检索模型，通过五个预训练编码器的参数无关融合构建，零额外训练即达到sub-500M模型最高MTEB分数；(2) B1ade-1B：SLM，在低成本GPU上使用GRPO算法在723M tokens（2.2M示例）的精选上下文-问题对上训练，奖励函数仅优化答案相似度，不包含显式引用监督。

Result: B1ade-1B在PopQA上达到81.82%，PubMedQA上65.8%，FEVER上51.09%。端到端RAG评估平均得分0.654（涵盖正确性、完整性、连贯性和忠实度），较SFT提升10.8%，并缩小了与1.5倍规模模型的差距。B1ade-embed零训练即达到sub-500M模型的顶级MTEB分数。

Conclusion: 接地行为（归因引用）无需显式奖励工程即可在RL训练中作为精度最大化策略涌现，这挑战了RAG系统需要大规模预训练和显式接地监督的传统假设。策略性模型组合和奖励设计足以实现资源高效的RAG。

Abstract: Language and embedding models used in RAG systems are conventionally assumed to require large-scale pretraining and explicit grounding supervision. We present B1ade, an efficient RAG architecture comprising two purpose-built components: a compact embedding model and a purpose-built SLM. B1ade-embed, a 335M parameter retrieval model constructed via parameter-free fusion of five pretrained encoders achieves top MTEB scores among sub-500M models with zero additional training, and B1ade-1B, an SLM trained on low-cost GPUs using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on 723M tokens (2.2M examples) of curated context-question pairs with rewards that optimize only answer similarity. Our central finding is emergent attribution: despite receiving no explicit supervision for source citation, B1ade-1B cites retrieved passages in 42.4% of responses, exceeding the attribution rate of its training distribution by 5.5 percentage points. This demonstrates that grounding behavior can emerge as an accuracy-maximizing strategy under RL training, without explicit reward engineering. On standard QA benchmarks, B1ade-1B achieves 81.82% on PopQA, 65.8% on PubMedQA, and 51.09% on FEVER. In end-to-end RAG evaluation, B1ade-1B achieves an average score of 0.654 across correctness, completeness, coherence, and faithfulness, a 10.8% improvement over the SFT, while closing the gap with models 1.5x its size. These results show that strategic model composition and reward design suffice for resource-efficient RAG, without large-scale pretraining.

</details>


### [120] [Belief Coevolution in a Social Network of Generalist and Specialist Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27512)
*Germans Savcisens,Samantha Dies,Courtney Maynard,Tina Eliassi-Rad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CoevolveSim框架，用于研究LLM在联网多智能体环境中的信念扩散机制，通过1,280次受控模拟发现：角色分配和网络结构显著重塑个体信念修正但对群体共识影响极小，而引入微调专家模型会使共识偏移翻倍以上并产生影响力不对称；简单基于持久性的观点动力学模型可复现全通用LLM群体行为，但异质群体需结合群体级信念组成与个体身份来预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被部署在多智能体环境中，但信念在相互作用的LLM之间形成和传播的过程仍不清楚，缺乏系统性的框架来隔离和研究所涉及的关键因素。

Method: 构建CoevolveSim框架，分离并研究三个因素：领域专业化、社会角色分配和社交网络结构。通用与专家LLM代理在每轮观察邻居信念摘要后更新自身信念。运行1,280次受控模拟，涵盖4种场景、2种网络结构和20条医学适应症陈述。采用简单基于持久性的观点动力学模型作为基线对照。

Result: 角色式分配和网络结构改变个体信念修正但对群体共识影响甚微；引入微调专家LLM使共识偏移翻倍以上并产生一致的影响力不对称；持久性观点动力学模型能复现全通用LLM群体的集体结果，但异质LLM群体需群体级信念组成来复现共识、需智能体身份来预测个体信念转变。

Conclusion: 在多智能体LLM系统中实现真实的信念扩散模拟需要多样化的基础LLM集合，仅依靠角色提示不足以达到该目标。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-agent environments. However, the processes by which beliefs form and propagate among interacting LLMs remain poorly understood. We introduce CoevolveSim, a framework for studying belief diffusion within networked LLM populations. CoevolveSim allows us to isolate and study three factors: domain specialization, social-role assignment, and social network structure. Within this framework, generalist and specialist LLM agents exchange and revise beliefs. In each round, an LLM agent observes a summary of its neighbors' beliefs before updating its own. We run 1,280 controlled simulations spanning four scenarios, two network structures, and 20 medical-indication statements. We find that persona-style role assignment and network structure reshape individual belief revision but have minimal effect on population-level consensus. In contrast, introducing (finetuned) specialist LLMs more than doubles the shift in consensus and gives rise to consistent asymmetries in exerted influence. We further show that simple persistence-based opinion-dynamics models reproduce collective outcomes in all-generalist LLM populations, whereas heterogeneous LLM populations require population-level belief composition to reproduce consensus and agent identity to predict individual belief transitions. Our results indicate that realistic simulation of belief diffusion in multi-agent LLM systems requires a diverse set of underlying LLMs, not persona prompting alone.

</details>


### [121] [Training Skills Like Parameters via Self-Supervised Semantic Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27557)
*Mo Li,Zixin Yin,Ting Cao,Yunxin Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督的自进化智能体框架，受扩散模型中"破坏-重建"范式的启发，通过对比智能体重建结果与人类原始作品构造自监督信号，为闭源大语言模型在创意编剧等专业领域提供无需权重访问和外部分数的能力提升路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在通用指令遵循上表现出色，但在创意编剧等高度专业化的开放式领域难以达到人类专家水平。现有后训练方法（SFT和RL）需要访问模型权重（闭源模型不可用）且计算开销大；而现有智能体持续学习方法依赖昂贵的人类专家标注或不可靠的LLM-as-a-judge反馈，存在显著瓶颈。

Method: 提出无监督自进化智能体框架，核心思想是借鉴扩散模型的"破坏-重建"（corruption-and-reconstruction）范式。利用现有高质量人类作品构造自监督信号：对原始作品进行破坏，让智能体进行重建，将重建结果与人类原始作品进行对比产生损失。与常规神经网训练不同，更新的不是模型权重，而是外部文本技能库。训练循环遵循前向、损失、反向的标准范式。

Result: 在短剧编剧这一具有挑战性的任务上评估，实验结果表明该框架能使智能体自主提取并内化高度可泛化的技能，显著增强其领域特定的生成能力。

Conclusion: 这种自对比反思范式为智能体在没有外部监督的情况下自主学习复杂高质量人类作品的生成提供了一条可扩展的路径，特别适用于无法访问权重的闭源模型。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable general instruction-following capabilities, they often fall short of human experts in highly specialized, open-ended domains such as creative screenwriting. Prior approaches typically adopt post-training, yet both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning require weight access that closed-source frontier models do not offer, and demand heavy compute. Moreover, what is learned is tied to a single checkpoint and cannot be inspected by humans. Recent advancements in agentic continual learning instead attempt to bridge this gap by accumulating external textual skills. However, these methods heavily rely on costly human expert annotations or unreliable LLM-as-a-judge feedback for reflection. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose a novel, unsupervised self-evolving agent framework inspired by the corruption-and-reconstruction paradigm of diffusion models. Instead of relying on explicit external scoring, we leverage existing high-quality human artifacts to construct self-supervised signals. Training then follows the familiar loop of neural network training, forward, loss, and backward, with the loss coming from contrasting the agent's reconstruction against the human original. What is updated is not model weights but an external library of textual skills. We evaluate our framework on the challenging task of short drama screenwriting. Experimental results demonstrate that our method enables the agent to autonomously extract and internalize highly generalizable skills, significantly enhancing its domain-specific generation capabilities. Furthermore, this self-contrastive reflection paradigm offers a scalable pathway for agents to teach themselves the production of complex, high-quality human artifacts, without requiring external supervision.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond Similarity: Grounded Agentic Extraction and Expert-Adjudicated Evaluation of Intertextuality in Classical Chinese Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27595)
*Zhaoji Wang,Wanyu Si,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将细粒度互文性提取重构为智能体任务：让大语言模型完整阅读两个文本单元，通过受限工具接口将每条文本复用关系锚定到精确字符跨度，并按五维类型学（形式、方面、来源标记、功能、立场）标注。作者以《论语》与《汉书》的穷尽比较构建了包含2,533对互文对的专家裁决基准，测试了12个LLM（精确率56%-93%，成本相差51倍），并将验证后的提取器扩展到二十四史（65,380次比较，5,766对），揭示出相似度分数无法表达的语料库级结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算互文性方法（从字符串匹配到神经检索）只能输出相似度分数和并行段落列表，能识别文本哪里复用，却无法刻画如何复用以及为何复用。作者希望超越"何处"的检测，迈向"如何与为何"的细粒度互文性特征刻画。

Method: 将细粒度互文性提取定义为智能体任务：LLM完整阅读两个文本单元后，通过受限工具接口将每条复用提议锚定在两侧的精确字符跨度上，并按五维类型学（形式、方面、来源标记、功能、立场）标注。构建流程为：多模型候选池+三位领域专家裁决，形成2,533对互文对的基准；在此标准上评估12个LLM的精确率、成本和置信度校准。

Result: 12个LLM精确率范围56%-93%；在可比质量下成本跨度达51倍；专家一致性展现出可靠性梯度：文本表层可见的维度标注一致，而需要意图推断的维度存在争议。扩展到二十四史后恢复出语料库级结构：引用的解释性构成在十八个世纪间无系统变化，但同一段落的引用越来越不字面化。

Conclusion: 聚合层面的稳定性与个体案例中的漂移并存，这符合文化吸引（cultural-attraction）理论的预期。作者发布了提取协议与专家裁决基准，为细粒度互文性分析提供了可复现的标准化框架。
