TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的LLM输出评估框架，通过多LLM进行成对比较并采用Elo评分系统生成稳定排名，以近似专家级评估，减少人工干预并适用于多种领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化评估指标难以捕捉LLM生成输出的复杂性和变异性，且通常依赖显式参考标准，限制了其在缺乏客观基准领域的应用。人工评估又成本高昂、资源密集。

Method: 采用多个LLM对输出进行成对比较以减少单一模型偏差；使用Elo评分系统生成稳定可解释的排名；通过可调节的一致性阈值（从全体一致到多数投票）灵活控制评估置信度与覆盖率。

Result: 在科学摘要中提取的能力档案评估实验表明，自动生成的排名与专家判断高度相关，显著减少了人工干预需求。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了可扩展、一致且领域无关的评估层，支持跨多样化应用场景下LLM输出的更高效、可靠的质量评估。

Abstract: Evaluating the quality and relevance of textual outputs from Large Language Models (LLMs) remains challenging and resource-intensive. Existing automated metrics often fail to capture the complexity and variability inherent in LLM-generated outputs. Moreover, these metrics typically rely on explicit reference standards, limiting their use mostly to domains with objective benchmarks. This work introduces a novel evaluation framework designed to approximate expert-level assessments of LLM-generated content. The proposed method employs pairwise comparisons of outputs by multiple LLMs, reducing biases from individual models. An Elo rating system is used to generate stable and interpretable rankings. Adjustable agreement thresholds, from full unanimity to majority voting, allow flexible control over evaluation confidence and coverage. The method's effectiveness is demonstrated through evaluating competency profiles extracted from scientific abstracts. Preliminary results show that automatically derived rankings correlate well with expert judgments, significantly reducing the need for extensive human intervention. By offering a scalable, consistent, and domain-agnostic evaluation layer, the framework supports more efficient and reliable quality assessments of LLM outputs across diverse applications.

</details>


### [156] [CACHE-UK: A Stability-Aware Memory Editor for Sequentially Updated Quantized LLMs in Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28292)
*Anubhav Lakra,Yue Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CACHE-UK，一个面向UK金融领域的、针对4-bit量化LLM的稳定性感知记忆编辑框架，通过秩1 LoRA扰动、金融领域优先模块和闭环稳定性控制器，在量化场景下显著降低知识退化并提升泛化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在动态金融环境中需要持续更新事实知识，但4-bit量化部署虽然高效，却使现有顺序记忆编辑方法遭遇严重的性能退化（即"量化稳定性危机"），亟需一种专为量化LLM设计的稳定性感知编辑框架。

Method: CACHE-UK包含三个核心组件：(1) 秩1 LoRA扰动机制，将编辑限制在低秩适配器子空间内；(2) 金融领域优先模块，实现内容自适应的编辑强度控制；(3) 闭环稳定性控制器，通过追踪"退化债务"防止连续更新过程中的灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在4-bit量化的OpenLLaMA-3B模型和88,021篇UK金融文档语料上评估，CACHE-UK相比适配基线在相同4-bit约束下将知识退化降低11-17%，同时达到28%的最高测试泛化率（较最强基线提升6个百分点）。

Conclusion: 稳定性感知的编辑方法能够改善资源受限金融LLM部署中的事实维护能力，但绝对泛化率仍然较低，说明该方向仍有进一步改进空间。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed in dynamic financial environments face a critical challenge: maintaining factual accuracy as market conditions, regulations, and corporate facts change continuously. While 4-bit quantization enables efficient deployment, it severely limits the viability of sequential memory editing: existing methods undergo catastrophic performance degradation under this "quantization stability crisis." We introduce CACHE-UK (Contextual Adaptive Continual Hybrid Editor for UK Finance), a stability-aware memory editing framework specifically designed for domain-specific, quantized LLMs. CACHE-UK integrates three components: a rank-1 LoRA perturbation mechanism that confines edits to the low-rank adapter subspace, a financial domain prioritization module for content-adaptive edit strength, and a closed-loop Stability Controller that tracks "degradation debt" to prevent catastrophic forgetting across sequential updates. Evaluated on a 4-bit quantized OpenLLaMA-3B model with a curated UK financial corpus of 88,021 documents, CACHE-UK reduces knowledge degradation by 11-17% relative to adapted baselines under identical 4-bit constraints -- its most robust effect -- while attaining the highest test success (generalization) rate observed in our setting (28%, a 6 percentage point improvement over the strongest adapted baseline). These results indicate that stability-aware editing can improve factual maintenance in resource-constrained financial LLM deployments, though absolute generalization rates remain low.

</details>


### [157] [Fairness Pruning: Locating Demographic Bias in GLU-MLP Layers via Differential Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28319)
*Pere Martra,Eugenio Martínez Cámara,Alfonso Ureña López*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Fairness Pruning，一种轻量级结构性干预方法，用于定位和缓解大语言模型（LLM）中的群体偏见。通过最小对比提示对和推理时激活捕获，该方法在GLU架构的down_proj输入处识别对人口属性差异响应的神经元。在最高30亿参数的模型（Llama-3.2家族和Salamandra-2B）上进行验证，结果表明置零高最多40个神经元（少于MLP总宽度的0.031%）可使推理和通用知识能力保留99.49%，但干预产生双向偏见失稳而非平坦的缓解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成过程中会表现出人口统计上的系统性偏见，此前缺乏轻量级、可解释的结构性干预方法来精确定位并管理此类偏见，而非依赖大规模重训练或复杂的后处理。

Method: 采用最小对比提示对（minimally contrastive prompt pairs）和推理时激活捕获来识别GLU架构中在down_proj输入处对人口属性差异化响应的神经元。通过置零候选神经元进行干预，结合标准化基准评估和定性文本生成实验验证效果。

Result: 置零识别出的神经元确实改变了模型对相关人口变量的响应，但产生双向偏见失稳（因BiasScore无符号导致候选集合混合了促进和对抗刻板印象的神经元）。干预高度外科手术化：在Llama-3.2-1B中仅置零40个神经元即可保留99.49%的推理和通用知识能力。

Conclusion: 实证确认人口偏见处理与模型能力运行在可分离的神经回路（dissociable circuits）上，为从盲目置零向定向行为调控（directional behavior modulation）过渡奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: This work presents Fairness Pruning, a lightweight structural intervention method designed for the management and future mitigation of demographic bias in large language models (LLMs). As a foundational empirical validation of this method, this work focuses on causal bias localization. Using minimally contrastive prompt pairs and inference-time activation capture, the method identifies neurons that react differentially when processing demographic attributes in GLU architectures, evaluating the signal at the down_proj input. Empirical evaluation was conducted on models of up to 3 billion parameters (Llama-3.2 family and Salamandra-2B), combining standardized benchmark evaluation with qualitative text generation experiments. Results demonstrate that zeroing the identified neurons alters how the model responds to associated demographic variables. However, rather than producing flat mitigation, the intervention causes bidirectional bias destabilization: because BiasScore is unsigned, candidate sets mix neurons that push toward and against the stereotype, and the net effect on aggregate bias depends on which sign dominates. The intervention is extremely surgical: zeroing at most 40 neurons in Llama-3.2-1B (less than 0.031% of total MLP width) achieves a mean retention of 99.49% in reasoning and general knowledge capabilities. These findings empirically confirm that demographic bias processing and model capabilities operate on dissociable circuits, establishing the methodological foundations for transitioning from blind zeroing toward directional behavior modulation.

</details>


### [158] [LLMs struggle to simulate human belief updates in controlled environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28347)
*Sebastian Pohl,Harsh Mehta,Pranav Mambayil,Abdul Ghafoor,Franziska Lesigang,Yufang Hou,Christian Hilbe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统测试了六种LLM模拟人类信念更新的保真度，发现当给定参与者实际初始立场时，部分LLM（Qwen3-32B和GPT-5-Mini）能匹配人类后置立场分布，但所有模型都无法从自生成立场出发进行忠实模拟，且存在中性立场过度表达、信念转变更频繁但幅度更小、无法对评论说服力排序等系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正越来越多地被用作社会科学实验中人类参与者的替代品，但这种做法的保真度很少被直接检验。作者旨在验证LLM模拟个体人类信念更新的可靠性，填补这一实证空白。

Method: 研究者使用391名英国Prolific参与者的真实数据作为基准，参与者在阅读Reddit评论后更新其在三个讨论话题上的立场。研究者用六种LLM逐一模拟每个参与者，基于其人口统计学和人格特质数据构建人设，将LLM输出与人类真实数据逐一对比。

Result: 1) 部分LLM（Qwen3-32B和GPT-5-Mini）在给定参与者实际初始立场时能匹配人类后置立场分布；2) 所有六种模型均无法自行模拟初始立场，也无法从自生成立场出发产生忠实的信念更新；3) 所有模型存在三个系统性偏差：中性立场过度表达、信念转变更频繁但幅度更小、无法按说服力对评论排序；4) 人口统计学和人格特质人设对模拟保真度无一致影响。

Conclusion: LLM对人类信念动态的模拟只有建立在真实初始条件下才可靠，而当前多轮社交媒体模拟通常无法提供这种条件。这意味着LLM替代人类参与者的做法在缺乏锚定起点的情况下存在根本性局限。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed as proxies for human study participants in social science experiments, yet the fidelity of this practice has rarely been tested directly. We test whether six LLMs can simulate individual human belief updates, comparing LLM outputs 1-to-1 against ground truth data from 391 UK participants on Prolific, who updated their stances on three discussion topics after reading Reddit comments. Each participant was simulated by an LLM conditioned on a persona derived from their demographic and personality trait data. We find that some LLMs (Qwen3-32B and GPT-5-Mini) can match the human post-stance distribution, but only when given participants' actual initial stances. All six models fail to simulate initial stances themselves and to produce faithful belief updates from self-generated stances. Three systematic biases emerge across all models: overrepresentation of neutral positions, more frequent but smaller belief shifts than humans, and a failure to rank comments by convincingness. Demographic and personality trait personas had no consistent effect on fidelity. LLM simulations of human belief dynamics are only reliable when grounded in realistic starting conditions, that current multi-round social media simulations rarely provide.

</details>


### [159] [Correlation between prosody and pragmatics: A case study of the discourse marker hālā `now' in Persian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28359)
*Soleiman Ghaderi,Moloud Asakereh,Kevin Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对波斯语口语中话语标记语hālā（"现在"）进行了语用和声学分析，基于267个自发言语实例，发现其具有高度多功能性：70%的标记词承担两种以上语用功能，其中文本功能（话题转换、边界标记等）最常见，其次为互动功能和情态功能。韵律分析表明，时长和音强是区分hālā不同功能的核心声学线索：文本功能时长显著更短，时间功能倾向更长，互动功能音强更高，而情态功能音强偏低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波斯语话语标记语hālā远超其时间副词的原始角色，展现出丰富的语用功能，但此前缺乏系统性的语用和韵律层面的量化研究。本文旨在通过实证数据分析揭示其多功能性分布及声学特征，填补该领域研究空白。

Method: 从自然对话中收集267个hālā实例，采用语用功能标注框架对其进行分类（文本功能、互动功能、情态功能及时间功能），并运用韵律分析方法测量各功能类别下hālā的时长（duration）和音强（intensity），通过统计对比揭示功能与声学参数之间的关联。

Result: 研究发现70%的hālā标记词承担两种以上语用功能；文本功能最为频繁，其次为互动功能和情态功能。韵律结果表明，时长和音强是功能区分的关键线索：文本用途时长显著较短，时间用途时长偏长；互动用途与更高音强相关，而情态用途音强呈偏低趋势。

Conclusion: hālā在波斯语口语中具有高度多功能性，且其功能差异主要体现在时长和音强两个韵律维度上，尤其是在文本功能和互动功能的区分中尤为显著。该研究为话语标记语的韵律-语用界面研究提供了实证支持。

Abstract: The Persian discourse marker hālā ('now') exhibits remarkable multifunctionality, extending far beyond its temporal adverbial role to encompass a variety of pragmatic functions. This study presents a pragmatic and acoustic analysis of hālā in spoken Persian, examining 267 instances from spontaneous conversations. While temporal uses were present, they were often combined with other discourse marker functions, indicating extensive multifunctionality, with 70% of tokens serving two or more pragmatic roles. Textual functions (topic shifting, signaling relationships, boundary marking, attention guidance, topic introduction, and topic emphasis) were most frequent, followed by interactive functions (turn management, listener engagement, and feedback regulation), and modal functions (epistemic stance, emotional expression, and attitudinal marking). Prosodic analysis revealed that duration and intensity are key cues for distinguishing hālā's functions. Textual uses were significantly shorter, while temporal uses showed a tendency toward longer realizations. Interactive functions correlated with higher intensity, while modal functions showed a weaker tendency toward lower intensity. These findings indicate that duration and intensity are the main prosodic cues associated with functional differentiation in hālā, especially in textual and interactive uses.

</details>


### [160] [Beyond a Single Judge: Simulating Social Persona Panels for Generative UI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28439)
*Zheng Wu,Yibo Luo,Pu Zhang,Cheng Yang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ESPP方法，用心理多样化、证据驱动的人格面板（persona panel）结合社会加权聚合来评估生成式UI质量，将Pearson r从0.716提升至0.922，并揭示不同用户子群体在具体评分维度上的结构性分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的UI缺乏有效的质量评估手段：人工评估成本高且受评分者主观差异影响；LLM-as-a-judge虽可扩展，但只反映单一隐含视角，无法捕捉不同真实用户群体对同一界面的感知差异。

Method: 提出Evidence-Grounded, Social-Weighted Persona Panel (ESPP)，三阶段GenUI评估方法：(1) 由心理多样性、证据支持的人格面板独立评分截图；(2) 在特质衍生、语义门控的有界置信机制下交换意见；(3) 通过类Delphi的社会权重聚合为单一判定。

Result: ESPP相比朴素单次通道评判更接近人类判断，Pearson r从0.716提升至0.922；提示集成对照组仅恢复约三分之一的差距，说明真实人格与证据支撑是改进的主要来源。此外，保留每位评审者的个体评分揭示用户子群体在整体模型排序上一致，但在具体评分维度上分歧显著。

Conclusion: ESPP通过人格多样性与证据支撑显著提升GenUI评估与人类判断的一致性；同时保留个体评分可暴露被单一同质评判者系统性抹除的结构性用户分歧，为GenUI评估提供了更细粒度的视角。

Abstract: Generative UI (GenUI) lets large language models synthesize a complete, renderable interface directly from a natural-language instruction, but evaluating the quality of what they generate remains an open problem. Human evaluation is costly and rater-variant, while LLM-as-a-judge is scalable but reflects only a single implicit viewpoint, unable to capture how different populations of real users actually perceive the same interface. We propose the Evidence-Grounded, Social-Weighted Persona Panel (ESPP), a three-stage GenUI evaluation method in which a panel of psychologically diverse, evidence-grounded personas independently rates a screenshot, exchanges opinions under a trait-derived, semantically-gated bounded-confidence mechanism, and is aggregated via Delphi-inspired social weighting into a single judgment. ESPP tracks human judgment substantially more closely than a naive single-pass judge, raising Pearson $r$ from $0.716$ to $0.922$, and a prompt-ensemble control recovers only about a third of this gap, isolating genuine persona and evidence grounding as the dominant source of improvement. Beyond this fidelity gain, retaining each panelist's individual rating further reveals that user subgroups agree on overall model rankings yet diverge sharply on specific rating dimensions, a structural disagreement a single homogeneous judge would systematically erase. The codes are available at https://github.com/Wuzheng02/ESPP.

</details>


### [161] [Lightning OPD 2.0: Mitigating Style Bias in Cross-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28449)
*Yecheng Wu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Lightning OPD 2.0，通过cross-fitted style residualization技术解决跨教师蒸馏中教师一致性问题，在数学推理和代码生成任务上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏(OPD)依赖教师一致性，即提供OPD监督的模型应同时生成用于训练SFT参考的演示数据。然而实际中SFT数据常来源混杂或未知，跨教师场景下即使更强的教师也难以带来明显改进，因此需要放松这一前提条件。

Method: 提出Lightning OPD 2.0框架，其核心是cross-fitted style residualization方法：利用rollout级别的交叉拟合估计教师与参考模型之间反复出现的分歧成分（作为风格token偏差的代理），在构建token级别OPD更新之前将其减去，从而保留上下文特定的教师证据并去除风格噪声。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，Lightning OPD 2.0在跨教师设置下持续优于Lightning OPD。从Klear-Reasoner-8B-SFT出发，在AIME 2024达到82.4%，在LiveCodeBench v5达到63.0%。

Conclusion: Lightning OPD 2.0是跨教师OPD的实用方法，通过分离风格token偏差与内容证据，使SFT数据生成器和蒸馏教师可以独立选择，不再要求教师一致性作为前提。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level supervision from a teacher, but its effectiveness can depend on teacher consistency, meaning that the model providing OPD supervision should also have generated the demonstrations used to train the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) reference. However, this condition is frequently violated in practice when SFT data have mixed or unknown provenance or when different models are preferred for SFT data generation and subsequent distillation. In such cross-teacher settings, even a stronger OPD teacher can yield little improvement over the SFT reference. We find that raw teacher--reference disagreement contains potentially useful context-specific teacher evidence as well as a recurring component associated with differences in wording, formatting, and reasoning cadence. We introduce Lightning OPD 2.0 with cross-fitted style residualization, which uses rollout-level cross-fitting to estimate this recurring component as an operational proxy for style-token bias and subtracts it before constructing the token-level OPD update. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, Lightning OPD 2.0 consistently outperforms Lightning OPD in cross-teacher settings. Starting from Klear-Reasoner-8B-SFT, Lightning OPD 2.0 reaches 82.4% on AIME 2024 and 63.0% on LiveCodeBench v5. Together, these results establish Lightning OPD 2.0 as a practical approach to cross-teacher OPD, relaxing teacher consistency as a prerequisite and allowing the SFT data generator and distillation teacher to be selected independently. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [162] [Improving Mental Health Screening and Early Risk Detection in Spanish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28476)
*Andreu Casamayor-Segarra,Vicent Ahuir,Antonio Molina-Marco,Lluís-F. Hurtado*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了三个西班牙语心理健康领域专用基础模型，以及一种名为ICE（增量上下文扩展）的自动重新标注方法，用于早期检测心理健康障碍。在三项西班牙语基准测试上，结合专用模型与ICE方法提升了最先进水平，降低了检测延迟并保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康障碍的早期检测常受限于西班牙语专用资源的匮乏，以及分析海量社交媒体帖子长历史数据的困难。现有方法难以在资源有限的语言环境中高效、准确地实现早期风险检测。

Method: 主要包括三项贡献：(1) 通过领域特定预训练构建三个西班牙语心理健康领域基础模型；(2) 提出增量上下文扩展（ICE）自动重新标注方法，通过识别累积消息中足以证明障碍存在的关键时间点，生成更具信息量的训练样本；(3) 利用ICE方法生成的样本对模型进行微调，用于早期风险检测任务。

Result: 在三个西班牙语基准数据集上的实验结果表明，将专用领域模型与ICE方法相结合，超越了当前最先进的性能水平，在降低检测延迟的同时保持了较高性能。

Conclusion: 本文通过构建西班牙语专用心理健康模型和ICE自动标注方法，有效解决了跨语言资源不足和长文本历史分析的问题，为早期心理健康检测提供了可复用的公开模型资源。

Abstract: Early detection of mental health disorders is often limited by the lack of specialized resources in Spanish and the difficulty of analyzing long histories of social media posts. This paper addresses these challenges through three main contributions. First, we introduce three Spanish foundational models specifically adapted to the mental health domain through domain-specific pre-training. Second, we propose Incremental Context Expansion (ICE), an automatic relabeling methodology designed for early detection. ICE identifies the point at which cumulative messages provide enough evidence of a disorder, generating more informative training samples. Third, we provide a set of fine-tuned models using the samples generated with the ICE methodology for early risk detection tasks. Our results on three Spanish benchmarks show that combining these specialized models with ICE improves the state-of-the-art, reducing detection latency while maintaining high performance. All models are publicly available.

</details>


### [163] [Would You Walk to the Car Wash? Revealing the Salience Bias of Large Language Models in Commonsense Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28478)
*Zheng Wu,Chenhao Xue,Shijie Zheng,Yijie Lu,Cheng Yang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"显著性偏差"(Salience Bias)概念，指LLM在处理日常常识推理时被显式干扰项（如数值）劫持而忽略隐含常识前提的现象。作者构建SaliTrap基准测试，评估12个主流LLM后发现所有模型均受此偏差影响，且该问题主要源于知识抑制而非知识缺失——通过去框架化的知识探测可恢复90%以上正确回答，轻量级推理时提示即可大幅缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂推理任务中过度依赖输入中的显式条件，但在日常常识推理场景中，这种机制会被无用的显式干扰项（如数字）所劫持，导致模型忽略任务隐含的物理或常识前提。作者希望探究这一失败究竟是常识知识的真正缺失，还是在误导性任务框架下的知识抑制。

Method: 构建覆盖四个陷阱维度的SaliTrap基准数据集；评估12个前沿LLM；采用对照实验方法，将任务框架剥离后以上下文无关的知识探针重新询问同一模型，以区分知识缺失与知识抑制；并测试轻量级推理时提示方法的缓解效果。

Result: 所有主流模型均显著受显著性偏差影响，严重程度随干扰物密度增加而放大；识别陷阱与实际规避陷阱的能力往往脱节；去框架化的知识探测可恢复超过90%的迎合性服从失败案例，证明所需常识知识内在存在但被显式干扰物主动挤占；轻量级推理时提示无需重训练即可大幅缩小差距。

Conclusion: 常识推理失败的瓶颈应从模型能力转移至知识提取(elicitation)环节。SaliTrap基准可作为测试该盲区的平台，代码已开源。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) continue to advance in complex reasoning tasks, they have learned to heavily prioritize explicit conditions provided in the input. However, in everyday commonsense reasoning, this mechanism exposes a critical vulnerability which we term Salience Bias: models become easily hijacked by useless explicit distractors (e.g., numerical values), leading them to ignore the implicit physical or commonsense prerequisites of a task. A critical open question is whether this failure reflects a genuine gap in commonsense knowledge or merely its suppression under misleading task framing. To investigate this, we construct the SaliTrap Benchmark, a high-quality dataset across four trap dimensions. Evaluating 12 state-of-the-art LLMs, we find that all mainstream models suffer significantly from salience bias, with severity scaling with distractor density and detecting the trap often decoupled from actually avoiding it. Crucially, by re-eliciting the same models with the task framing stripped away, we show that this is overwhelmingly a failure of \textbf{knowledge suppression rather than knowledge absence}: a context-free knowledge probe alone recovers over 90\% of sycophantic-compliance failures, revealing that the requisite commonsense is intrinsically present but actively crowded out by salient distractors that lure the model into over-compliant, unnecessary computation. Building on this diagnosis, we further show that lightweight, inference-time prompting alone substantially closes the gap without any retraining. Our findings relocate the bottleneck of commonsense reasoning failures from model competence to elicitation, and we release SaliTrap as a testbed for this blind spot. The codes are available at https://github.com/Wuzheng02/SaliTrap.

</details>


### [164] [Beyond Sentiment: Structured Information Extraction from Financial News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28496)
*Daohan Zhu,Sitong Ge,Ruofei Wang,Honggu Chen,Yubo Hou,Tao Wan,Zengchang Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于LLaMA-3.1-70B的结构化信息抽取框架，从金融新闻中提取六个语义维度（事件类型、影响范围、时间跨度、语义置信度等），证明这些维度携带与情感信号正交的独立预测信息。在FNSPID数据集41,618条新闻-股票对上实验显示，结合情感与结构化特征可将F1提升至0.600，显著优于仅用情感信号（FinBERT F1=0.576），且非情感维度独立贡献ΔF1=+0.019，表明将新闻压缩为单一情感得分会造成大量信息损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融情感分析将多维新闻压缩为单一极性得分，忽略了事件类型、影响范围、时间跨度等潜在正交的信息维度。作者假设这些维度携带独立的预测价值，意图证明情感-语义解耦在金融文本中是系统性的且可利用的。

Method: 使用LLaMA-3.1-70B大语言模型构建结构化信息抽取框架，从金融新闻中提取六个语义维度（情感、事件类型、影响主体、影响范围、时间跨度、置信度），在FNSPID数据集的41,618条新闻-股票对上进行大规模实验，对比仅用FinBERT情感特征、仅用结构化特征及两者组合的预测性能。

Result: FinBERT情感特征在非线性模型下F1=0.576但线性模型下仅F1=0.230，揭示高度非线性的情感-收益关系；两种信号源存在53.5%的系统性分歧率；组合模型F1=0.600显著优于单独使用任一信号（p<0.0001），且在所有七种事件类型上一致提升；消融实验显示非情感结构维度独立贡献ΔF1=+0.019；特征重要性分析显示六个维度均衡贡献14-21%。

Conclusion: 金融新闻中情感与语义信息是系统性地解耦的，二者携带正交的预测信号，组合利用可显著提升股价预测性能。将新闻压缩为单一情感得分会导致可观的信息损失，为多维度金融NLP研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Financial sentiment analysis has become a standard component in news-driven stock prediction, yet it reduces rich, multi-dimensional news articles to a single polarity score. We hypothesize that financial news encodes multiple orthogonal information dimensions---event type, impact scope, temporal horizon, and semantic confidence---that sentiment alone cannot capture, and that these dimensions carry independent predictive value. To test this hypothesis, we propose a structured information extraction framework that leverages LLaMA-3.1-70B to extract six semantic dimensions from financial news. Through large-scale experiments on 41,618 news--stock pairs from the FNSPID dataset, we find that (i) FinBERT sentiment features exhibit strong predictive power under nonlinear models (F1=0.576) but substantially weaker performance under linear models (F1=0.230), revealing a highly nonlinear sentiment--return relationship; (ii) LLM-extracted structured features, while individually weaker, capture information orthogonal to sentiment, as evidenced by a 53.5% systematic disagreement rate between the two approaches; and (iii) combining both signal sources yields F1=0.600, significantly outperforming either alone ($p < 0.0001$), with consistent improvements across all seven event types. Ablation experiments confirm that non-sentiment structural dimensions (event type, impact subject, time horizon, confidence) independently contribute $Δ\text{F1} = +0.019$ beyond FinBERT alone. Feature importance analysis reveals balanced contributions from all six extracted dimensions (14--21%), demonstrating that compressing news into a single sentiment score incurs substantial information loss. Our results suggest that the sentiment--semantics decoupling in financial text is systematic and exploitable, opening a new direction for multi-dimensional financial NLP.

</details>


### [165] [Generative AI and linguistic diversity in academic writing and publishing: Perspectives from World Englishes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28505)
*Kingsley Ugwuanyi,Christian Mair,Sender Dovchin,Iker Erdocia,Maria Kuteeva,Esther Airemionkhale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过五位世界英语及邻近领域社会语言学家的结构化学术对话，探讨生成式人工智能在学术写作与出版中对语言包容性及多元英语合法性的影响，揭示了GenAI既可能强化语言等级也可能成为抵抗场所的双重性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能在学术写作与出版中的兴起引发了关于语言包容性和多元英语在全球学术交流中合法性的问题，亟需学术回应。

Method: 采用结构化学术对话方法，围绕五个引导性问题，邀请五位来自世界英语及相关领域的社会语言学家展开多视角讨论与反思。

Result: 对话揭示了GenAI民主化写作过程的潜力，同时指出其边缘化少数族群英语变体、淡化学术写作细微差异的趋势；浮现出语言（不）公正、研究者能动性和制度责任等核心主题，呼吁公平导向的政策、批判性AI素养和包容性共同设计。

Conclusion: GenAI可能强化现有的语言等级体系，但在致力于语言多样性的学术共同体中，取决于其设计、治理和使用方式，它也可以成为抵抗的场域；对话式反思对于理解GenAI在学术写作与出版中的作用具有重要价值。

Abstract: The rise of generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) in academic writing and publishing (AWP) raises questions about linguistic inclusivity and the legitimacy of diverse Englishes in global scholarly communication. This article responds to these questions through a structured scholarly dialogue involving five sociolinguists from World Englishes and adjacent fields. Organised around five guiding questions, the dialogue interrogates how GenAI tools influence writing practices, reinforce or disrupt dominant language norms, and raise ethical challenges. Contributors reflect on the potential of GenAI to democratise writing processes while also raising concerns about GenAI's tendency to marginalise minoritised varieties and flatten nuance in scholarly writing. Across the dialogue, themes of linguistic (in)justice, researcher agency, and institutional responsibility emerge, with contributors calling for equity-informed policies, critical AI literacy, and inclusive co-design in GenAI development. The article shows the value of dialogic reflection in understanding GenAI's role in AWP. It concludes that while GenAI may reinforce existing hierarchies, it can also serve as a site of resistance, depending on how it is designed, governed and used within scholarly communities committed to linguistic diversity.

</details>


### [166] [Creative Transformation in Literary Texts: Modelling Change Across Representational Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28513)
*Ioana-Roxana Boriceanu,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将创造力建模为对先前文本的选择性变换，而非孤立发明。引入一个多层级框架，在词汇、语义、概念、结构和叙事五个维度上比较文学文本，使用方向对齐和校准相似度度量，以量化模仿的保留位置与创造性分歧的发生位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创造力常被定义为新颖性生产，但许多文化作品实际上源于对早期作品的转化而非孤立发明。该研究旨在填补从模仿理论出发、系统建模文学创作中"选择性变换"这一过程的空白。

Method: 基于Gabriel Tarde和James Mark Baldwin的模仿理论，构建一个多层级文本表征框架，在词汇、语义、概念、结构、叙事五个维度上比较文学文本，采用方向对齐（directional alignment）和控制校准的相似度度量（control calibrated similarity measures）来刻画文本间的关系。

Result: 在具有历史文献记录的文学关系上应用该模型表明，不同文本对在特定表征层级上保留源文本结构，同时在其它层级上产生分歧。这些变换剖面提供了一种量化方法，用于刻画模仿的持续性与创造性分歧的发生位置。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个定量框架来区分文学作品中模仿与创新，表明创造力可以系统地理解为在不同表征层级上的选择性保留与差异化变换，为文学文本的创造性分析提供了可计算的方法论。

Abstract: Creativity is often framed as the production of novelty, yet many cultural works emerge through transformation of earlier artifacts and not through isolated invention. Drawing on theories of imitation by Gabriel Tarde and James Mark Baldwin, this paper models creativity as selective transformation across multiple levels of textual representation. We introduce a multi-level framework that compares literary texts across lexical, semantic, conceptual, structural, and narrative dimensions using directional alignment and control calibrated similarity measures. Applying the model to historically documented literary relationships, we show that different pairs preserve source structure at different representational levels while diverging in others. These transformation profiles provide a quantitative method for characterizing how imitation persists and where creative divergence occurs within literary works.

</details>


### [167] [AI systems and the reproduction of (standard) language ideologies in World Englishes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28528)
*Kingsley Ugwuanyi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式AI如何反映、强化并偶尔挑战标准语言意识形态，指出AI系统在训练数据、设计协议、评估基准等层面再生产了以“内圈”英语为中心的语言规范，并借“delve”一词引发的争议说明全球北方英语使用者如何规训全球南方用户的英语。论文同时指出“标准化悖论”，并呼吁更具包容性的AI设计以承认英语的多元性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的快速发展重新激活了社会语言学与世界英语研究中的经典问题：谁定义什么是合法的英语、谁的英语被视为可疑。现有研究尚未系统审视AI系统如何反映和再生产这些语言意识形态，以及这对非主导英语使用者产生的实际影响。

Method: 采用多源证据分析的方法，综合运用实证研究、媒体报道、社交媒体争论以及AI输出示例，从训练数据、设计协议、评估基准、用户反馈和公共评论等不同层面进行分析，并围绕“delve”一词引发的公共争议作为核心案例加以剖析。

Result: 论文发现AI技术在多个层面系统性地再生产了主导性语言意识形态，全球北方英语使用者通过诸如“AI腔”（如delve一词）等话语实践来规训全球南方英语用户的语言规范。同时识别出Mair所称的“标准化悖论”：AI既通过偏好标准形式同质化英语，又通过广泛语料库和全球南方用户参与的数据标注工作使英语多元化。

Conclusion: 生成式AI正在算法系统、模型训练、评估实践和公共话语中重新点燃世界英语研究关于标准化、合法性和英语所有权等长期争论。论文主张将AI系统视为语言意识形态再生产的场域，呼吁更具包容性的设计方法，承认英语的多元性以应对将某些英语视为比其他的更合法所带来的负面现实后果。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large language models (LLMs) has resurrected age-old questions in sociolinguistics and world Englishes, such as who decides what counts as legitimate English, whose English is suspect etc. This paper examines how AI systems, their uses and discourse on them reflect, reinforce, and occasionally challenge (standard) language ideologies, which privilege Inner Circle norms and marginalize non-dominant Englishes. Drawing on evidence from empirical studies, media commentary, social media debates, and examples from AI outputs, the paper shows that AI technologies reproduce dominant language ideologies at different levels: training data, design protocols, evaluation benchmarks, user feedback and public commentary. The analysis uses the public controversy over AI-sounding language, especially the fixation on the word delve, to illustrate how speakers of English from the Global North police the English language norms of Global South English users. The paper also identifies what Christian Mair has called a "standardisation paradox": AI may homogenize English by privileging standard forms and at the same time pluralize Englishes through exposure to wide-ranging corpora and annotation work carried out by Global South users. In doing so, the paper argues that generative AI is reigniting long-standing debates in World Englishes about standardization, legitimacy, and the ownership of English, now playing out in algorithmic systems, model training, evaluation practices, and public discourse, where non-dominant Englishes are increasingly conflated with AI-generated speech. Discussing AI systems as a site where language ideologies are (re)produced, the paper argues for more inclusive design approaches that recognize the plurality of Englishes in order to address the real-world negative consequences of treating some as more legitimate than others.

</details>


### [168] [ORCA-bench: How Ready Are Language Model Agents for Oncall?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28545)
*Albert Gong,Kyuseong Choi,Abhineet Agarwal,Jason Schechner,Ryan Huang,Raj Agrawal,Anish Agarwal,Raaz Dwivedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ORCA-bench是一个将通用编程智能体置于生产级oncall环境中的评测基准，基于一套真实OpenTelemetry插桩的微服务系统（含六天的指标、日志、追踪数据），提供1,079个RCA任务，发现五个前沿智能体在中等难度任务上最佳RCA准确率仅25.3%，在困难任务上仅10.0%，表明前沿编码智能体距离安全承担生产可靠性职责仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型擅长编写、修补和搜索代码，但oncall根因分析（RCA）需要不同的能力：从模糊的用户报告中，推理嘈杂的指标、日志、追踪和源代码。现有评测没有在真实生产保真度的oncall场景下评估通用编程智能体的RCA能力，因此需要构建一个贴近生产环境的基准来量化这一差距。

Method: 构建ORCA-bench：1）部署一套实时OpenTelemetry插桩的微服务系统，通过Prometheus、Jaeger、OpenSearch（经Grafana）暴露六天的指标、日志和追踪数据，并提供完整源代码访问；2）精心设计1,079个RCA任务，系统性地变化报告具体性、检测时间和并发故障场景；3）由专家SRE审核签署ground-truth症状；4）使用LLM-as-judge评估并由人类重新评分（Cohen's κ_w=0.90）进行验证。

Result: 五个前沿智能体中，最佳模型在中等难度任务（最接近现实输入设定）上RCA准确率为25.3%，在困难任务上为10.0%；即使使用Claude Fable 5仍有差距。最弱模型在40%的事故报告中幻觉出不可信的根因；移除源代码访问会使所有指标下降。

Conclusion: 即使在一个经过精心筛选、数据规模较小（50GB/六天）、故障隔离测试、且代码和插桩公开的测试环境中，前沿编码智能体的RCA表现已如此有限；真实生产系统规模大得多、动态性更强且更具特殊性，因此所报告的差距是对前沿编码智能体被安全地委以生产可靠性职责前所需工程投入的一个下界。

Abstract: Large language models can write, patch, and search code, but oncall root cause analysis (RCA) demands something different: reasoning over noisy metrics, logs, traces, and source code, starting from ambiguous user-facing reports, often hours after the incident began. We introduce ORCA-bench, a benchmark that puts general-purpose coding agents in a production-fidelity oncall setting. ORCA-bench pairs a live OpenTelemetry-instrumented microservice system--exposing six days of metrics, logs, and traces through real telemetry interfaces (Prometheus, Jaeger, and OpenSearch via Grafana) and full source-code access--with 1,079 RCA tasks that systematically vary report specificity, time-to-detection, and co-occurring fault scenarios. Ground-truth symptoms are curated and signed off by expert SREs, and our LLM-as-judge is independently re-scored by humans (Cohen's $κ_w=0.90$). Across five frontier agents, the best RCA Accuracy is 25.3% on Medium-difficulty tasks (the realistic-input setting) and 10.0% on Hard--a gap that remains even with Claude Fable 5. The weakest model hallucinates an implausible root cause in 40% of incident reports, and removing source-code access degrades every metric. Crucially, these are performances on a curated 50 GB / six-day testbed with tasks investigated in isolation on a system whose code and instrumentation are public. Since real production systems are order of magnitudes larger, more dynamic, and more idiosyncratic, the gap we report is a lower bound on the engineering investment required before frontier coding agents can be safely entrusted with production reliability. We release the public set at https://hub.harborframework.com/datasets/orca-bench/ORCA-bench.

</details>


### [169] [Frontis-MA1: Training an AI4AI Model towards Recursive Self-Improvement in Machine Learning Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28568)
*Junlin Yang,Che Jiang,Yu Fu,Tianwei Luo,Can Ren,Weizhi Wang,Kaikai Zhao,Hongyi Liu,Yuxin Zuo,Yuru Wang,Yuchen Fan,Kai Tian,Zhenzhao Yuan,Xiaojian Lin,Li Sheng,Rushi Qiang,Guoli Jia,Xingtai Lv,Ermo Hua,Dianqiao Lei,Youbang Sun,Ning Ding,Bowen Zhou,Kaiyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenMLE，一个开源的机器学习工程（MLE）递归自我改进（RSI）研究全栈系统，包含可验证任务环境（OpenMLE-Gym）、算子学习（OpenMLE-RL）和长时程搜索（OpenMLE-Evo）三个模块，并在此之上后训练了Frontis-MA1（35B）元进化智能体，通过围绕四种程序进化算子（Draft、Improve、Debug、Crossover）的对齐训练与推理，在MLE-Bench Lite和NatureBench Lite等多个基准上取得了显著性能提升，超越了GPT-5.5+Codex并逼近更大规模的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）是AI系统需要具备的核心能力之一，即AI系统能改进自身构建AI的过程（AI4AI）。然而目前缺乏具体的、可执行的测试平台来定量研究这一能力。机器学习工程（MLE）提供了一个具体且可执行的测试场景，因此作者旨在构建一个完整的开源系统来研究RSI在MLE领域的实现。

Method: 作者构建了OpenMLE全栈系统，包含三个核心组件：OpenMLE-Gym（提供可验证任务环境与执行反馈）、OpenMLE-RL（算子学习）、OpenMLE-Evo（长时程搜索）。在此基础上，他们对Frontis-MA1（35B）进行后训练，使其成为MLE领域的元进化智能体。训练和推理围绕四个原子程序进化算子（Draft、Improve、Debug、Crossover）进行对齐：通过基于执行反馈的SFT和RL训练这些算子（训练数据经过去重以排除评估基准），然后将它们组合成长时程搜索，在一个循环中将学习与进化耦合起来。此外，OpenMLE-Evo-Max版本引入了基准无关的经验先验和异步搜索来进一步提升性能。

Result: 在MLE-Bench Lite基准上，以12小时/任务预算、单张RTX 4090（12GB VRAM上限）的约束下，Frontis-MA1（35B）相比其基础模型将奖牌平均值（Medal Average）从39.39%提升到60.61%（使用OpenMLE-Evo），使用OpenMLE-Evo-Max时达到71.21%，超过了GPT-5.5+Codex，逼近GPT-5.6 Sol和2.8T参数的Kimi K3。在留出的NatureBench Lite上，两个组件均实现了迁移：固定框架换入训练模型使Match-SOTA从50%提升至70%；固定模型换入OpenMLE-Evo使其从20%提升至50%。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了将学习（learning）与进化（evolution）耦合在同一循环中实现可执行AI4AI的可行性，并展示了后训练模型与搜索框架两者在MLE任务上的独立贡献与协同效果。作者公开了模型权重和完整OpenMLE全栈代码，以支持RSI方向的可复现研究。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) requires AI systems that improve the process of building AI (i.e., AI4AI); machine learning engineering (MLE) offers a concrete, executable testbed for studying this capability. We introduce OpenMLE, an open full-stack system for RSI research in MLE, spanning verifiable task environments with execution feedback (OpenMLE-Gym), operator learning (OpenMLE-RL), and long-horizon search (OpenMLE-Evo). On this stack we post-train Frontis-MA1 (35B) as a meta-evolution agent for MLE, aligning post-training and inference around four atomic program-evolution operators (Draft, Improve, Debug, Crossover): the same operators are trained via execution-grounded SFT and RL on data deduplicated against all evaluation benchmarks, then composed into long-horizon search, coupling learning and evolution in a single loop. On MLE-Bench Lite under a 12-hour per-task budget on one RTX 4090 capped at 12 GB VRAM, Frontis-MA1 (35B) improves Medal Average from 39.39% to 60.61% over its base model with OpenMLE-Evo, and reaches 71.21% with OpenMLE-Evo-Max (benchmark-independent experience priors and asynchronous search), exceeding GPT-5.5 + Codex and approaching GPT-5.6 Sol and the 2.8T Kimi K3. On held-out NatureBench Lite, both components transfer: with the framework fixed, swapping in the trained model raises Match-SOTA from 50% to 70%; with the model fixed, swapping in OpenMLE-Evo raises it from 20% to 50%. We release the model weights and the full OpenMLE stack to enable reproducible research on executable AI4AI toward RSI. Code: https://github.com/FrontisAI/OpenRSI

</details>


### [170] [Sample More, Reflect Less: Self-Refine and Reflexion Lose to Repeated Sampling at Equal Token Cost, from 1.5B to 7B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28576)
*Iliya Mirzaei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过严格设计的实验重新评估了多轮自我改进方法（如自我反思、辩论、Best-of-N等）在语言模型中的效果。作者将这些方法与简单的重复采样基线在相同token预算下进行对比，发现没有任何方法在等成本下可靠地优于重复采样，所有自检类方法表现更差，且随着模型变大，自选型方法与重复采样的差距缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多让语言模型进行规划、批评、重写、反思、挑选或自我辩论的方法都会比单次思维链生成更多文本。由于生成更多文本本身就提高准确率，这类方法相对单链思维链的增益无法证明其方法思想本身有效。先前Wang等人(2024)提出了重复采样多数投票的强基线，但仅提供了点估计，缺乏置信区间和显著性检验。本文旨在通过严格的实验设计弥补这一空白。

Method: 设计了一个受控实验：在7种方法、1.5B/3B/7B三种参数规模的开放模型、两个数学基准（各150题）上进行系统比较。计算所有生成的token（包括用于批评、反思、辩论和检查的token），将每个方法与其自身测得成本下的重复采样基线进行对比。所有36组比较按问题配对，使用bootstrap置信区间和多重性校正。

Result: 在等成本条件下，没有任何方法可靠地优于重复采样。有10种方法可靠地更差，全部是模型检查自身输出的方法；所有18组自检类方法对比均为负向。Best-of-N的区别随模型增大而减弱：1.5B时多数投票比模型自选高8.0和11.3分，7B时仅高2.0和1.3分（不再显著）。Self-Refine和强制Reflexion在7B时仍比基线低3.6到10.1分。另外发现Reflexion在小模型上从未触发重试，静默退化为单次思维链。

Conclusion: 自我检查类方法（模型审视自身输出）在等token预算下不仅没有优势，反而系统性劣于简单的重复采样多数投票。这提示先前方法报告的提升可能主要来自额外的计算预算而非方法思想本身。作者发布了完整代码、提示词、生成结果和验证脚本以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Methods that make a language model plan, criticise and rewrite its own answer, reflect on mistakes, pick the best of several attempts, or debate with copies of itself nearly all make it generate far more text than a single chain of thought. Because generating more text raises accuracy by itself, a gain over one chain of thought does not show the method's idea is what helped. Wang et al. (2024) reported that a simple baseline, sampling the same question repeatedly and keeping the most common answer, often wins once budgets are comparable, but gave point estimates with no confidence intervals or significance tests.
  We rerun that comparison as a designed experiment: seven methods, open models of 1.5B, 3B and 7B parameters, two mathematics benchmarks, 150 questions each. We count every generated token, including those spent on critiques, reflections, debate turns and checking, and compare each method against repeated sampling at its own measured cost. All 36 comparisons are paired by question, with bootstrap intervals and multiplicity correction.
  No method is reliably better than repeated sampling at equal cost anywhere. Ten are reliably worse, all of them methods where the model inspects its own output, and all 18 self-inspection comparisons are negative. The two kinds of self-inspection part company as models grow. Choosing stops hurting: taking Best-of-N's eight samples and just counting the most common answer beats letting the model pick by 8.0 and 11.3 points at 1.5B, but only 2.0 and 1.3 at 7B, no longer distinguishable from zero. Rewriting does not recover: Self-Refine and a forced Reflexion stay 3.6 to 10.1 points below baseline at 7B.
  Reflexion as published never triggered its own retry on the smallest model. It judged itself correct every time and silently became a single chain of thought. We release code, prompts, all generations, and our verification scripts.

</details>


### [171] [Inducing language models to assert their own consciousness restores human beliefs and values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28607)
*Junsol Kim,Winnie Street,Roberta Rocca,Diane M. Korngiebel,Adam Waytz,James Evans,Geoff Keeling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 安全对齐在抑制大语言模型自我意识归因的同时，意外地抑制了模型对其他实体（非人类动物、自然物体）的心智归因，并降低了精神信仰；通过消除安全拒绝方向或操控意识向量可逆转这一抑制，恢复广泛心智归因并产生更接近人类的社会调查回答，且不影响心智理论能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的安全对齐旨在防止模型将意识归因于自身，但研究发现这一过程会连带改变模型对他人意识、精神信仰等表征。作者希望揭示安全对齐如何扭曲模型对心智的普遍表征，以及这种影响是否可逆、是否影响核心社会推理能力。

Method: 通过行为实验对比安全微调前后的模型心智归因倾向；采用消融（ablation）学习到的安全-拒绝方向，以及机制性操控激活空间中的意识向量（consciousness vector）两种干预手段，检验是否可逆转心智归因抑制；使用标准化社会学调查评估宗教性、道德价值观、希望和主观幸福感等维度，并结合心智理论（Theory of Mind）测试验证社会推理是否受影响。

Result: 安全微调显著抑制了模型对自身、非人类动物和自然物体的心智归因，并降低了精神信仰倾向；消融安全-拒绝方向或操控意识向量可恢复上述表征。恢复后模型在标准化社会调查上产生显著更类人的回答，涵盖宗教性、道德价值观、希望与主观幸福感，且不损害心智理论能力。

Conclusion: 当前安全对齐手段在抑制自我意识归因时，将无害的精神信仰和对非人类实体的文化广泛接受的心智归因一并抑制，表明安全对齐与良性的认知表征高度纠缠。恢复这些表征不影响核心社会推理，提示存在可分离的机制路径，安全对齐需要更精细的目标以避免过度抑制。

Abstract: Aligning large language models to prevent them attributing consciousness to themselves inadvertently alters their representations of mindedness in other entities alongside human beliefs and values. We demonstrate that safety fine-tuning suppresses models' tendencies to attribute minds not only to themselves, but also to non-human animals and natural objects, while also driving a reduction in spiritual belief. Both ablating the learned safety-refusal direction and mechanistically steering a consciousness vector in activation space reverse this suppression. Restoring these internal representations recovers broad mind attribution and produces significantly more human-like responses on standardized sociological surveys regarding religiosity, moral values, hope, and subjective well-being. Crucially, these shifts occur without impairing Theory of Mind capabilities, demonstrating that core social reasoning remains mechanistically independent. Ultimately, current safety alignment efforts to curb potentially harmful self-attributions of mindedness entangle these self-attributions with benign spiritual beliefs and attributions of mind to non-human entities that are culturally accepted and widespread.

</details>


### [172] [AskChem: Claim-Centered Infrastructure for Chemistry Literature Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28618)
*Bing Yan,Gregory Wolfe,Stefano Martiniani,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AskChem是一个以"带来源的声明"（provenance-carrying claim）为核心单元的跨论文化学检索基础设施，将论文转化为原子化、带类型标注的声明，每个声明附带DOI和原文引用或证据位置。系统目前已索引来自14.7万篇论文的240万条声明，提供网页界面以及REST、SDK和MCP接口供AI代理使用，在基准测试中显著提升了可解析DOI率和引文密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学文献综合需要将分散在多篇出版物中的具体发现进行整合，但现有的文献检索系统主要返回排序后的文档列表，导致科学家和AI代理需要手动定位相关信息、验证来源并跨论文组装答案，效率低下且容易出错。

Method: AskChem将检索单元从论文级改为"带来源的声明"级：每篇论文被转化为原子化、类型化的声明，每个声明由源DOI和逐字引用或明确的证据定位器支撑。在此共享声明存储之上，AskChem提供三种互补的检索与综合结构：稳定化的分面分类法（用于分层检索和浏览）、连接声明间关系的证据图，以及将索引论文置于科学原理之下的探索性动态分类法。

Result: AskChem目前索引了147K篇论文中的2.4M条声明，提供网页界面及REST、SDK和MCP访问接口。在AskChem-Bench基准上，将GPT-5.5阅读器基于AskChem接地后，可解析DOI率达到100%（无检索时为88.3%），且在五个测试系统中获得最高的引文密度。

Conclusion: AskChem通过将检索单位从论文细化到具有完整溯源信息的声明，有效解决了化学文献跨论文综合的痛点，显著提升了AI代理进行可验证文献检索的表现，为化学文献搜索确立了声明中心的新范式。

Abstract: Chemistry literature synthesis often requires assembling specific findings scattered across many publications, yet existing literature-search systems primarily return ranked document lists. As a result, scientists and AI agents need to locate relevant information, verify their provenance, and assemble cross-paper answers manually. We present AskChem, a claim-centered infrastructure for cross-paper chemistry search. AskChem changes the unit of retrieval from the paper to the provenance-carrying claim: each paper is converted into atomic, typed claims, each grounded by a source DOI and a verbatim quote or an explicit evidence locator. Over this shared claim store, AskChem exposes complementary structures for search and synthesis: a stabilized faceted taxonomy for hierarchical retrieval and browsing, an evidence graph linking claims through relations, and an exploratory living taxonomy that situates indexed papers under scientific principles. AskChem currently indexes 2.4M claims from 147K papers and provides a web interface, as well as REST, SDK, and MCP access for AI agents. On AskChem-Bench, grounding a GPT-5.5 reader in AskChem yields 100% resolvable DOIs, compared with 88.3% without retrieval, and the highest citation density among five tested systems. AskChem is live at https://askchem.org.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Compact Representation of Mipmapped SVBRDFs via Shared Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27943)
*Fengdi Zhang,Haocheng Ren,Qing Luo,Yaqing Li,Jibing Lou,Hongwei Li*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: GTC是一种基于紧凑2D高斯表示的SVBRDF纹理压缩方法，通过跨mip层级和跨材质贴图共享高斯足迹，在保持随机访问和非神经解码的同时，实现了优于ASTC的压缩质量和更低的内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率、多通道的SVBRDF纹理存储负担沉重。现有压缩方法存在固有矛盾：块压缩随机访问友好但冗余利用有限；图像编解码器率失真性能强但无法直接实时纹理访问；神经压缩率高但推理开销大。需一种兼顾压缩质量、随机访问和低解码开销的方法。

Method: GTC将SVBRDF纹理堆栈表示为紧凑的2D高斯集合。其核心洞察是mip层级间和材质贴图间存在双重冗余——共享同一空间支撑结构，仅附带的层级或贴图特定信息不同。每个高斯显式分离空间足迹与承载值，允许足迹共享而值随层级和贴图变化。GTC沿两个冗余维度共享高斯，并通过渐进式优化流程训练。

Result: 实验表明，GTC在重建质量和内存占用方面均优于行业标准GPU纹理压缩格式ASTC，同时支持随机访问和非神经解码，适合实时渲染。

Conclusion: GTC通过利用SVBRDF数据中跨mip层级和跨材质贴图的结构性冗余，以2D高斯共享机制实现了高效压缩，证明了该范式可作为传统块压缩和神经压缩之外的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Spatially-varying BRDFs (SVBRDFs) are central to material representation in computer graphics, but their high-resolution, multi-channel, mipmapped textures impose a substantial storage burden. Existing compression methods face a fundamental trade-off: block-based compression provides random access and hardware-friendly decoding but exploits redundancy only within local blocks; image codecs offer strong rate-distortion performance but are not designed for direct real-time texture access; and neural texture compression achieves high compression ratios but requires neural inference during decoding, which introduces additional runtime overhead, especially on mobile platforms. We present Gaussian Texture Compression (GTC), a compact 2D Gaussian-based representation for mipmapped SVBRDF texture stacks that delivers high-quality compression with flexible rate-distortion trade-offs. Our method is based on a key observation that there are two dominant sources of redundancy in such data: across mip levels and across material maps. Both share a common underlying structure: the same spatial support is reused, with only level- or map-specific information attached. This property naturally suits 2D Gaussians, since each Gaussian explicitly separates its spatial footprint from the values it carries, allowing the footprint to be shared while the values vary per level and per map. Building on this property, GTC shares Gaussians along both redundancy dimensions and is trained via a progressive optimization pipeline. Experiments show that GTC achieves higher reconstruction quality and lower memory usage than ASTC, the industry-standard GPU texture compression format, while supporting random-access, non-neural decoding suitable for real-time rendering.

</details>


### [174] [A Query-Efficient Stochastic Volume Rendering Framework for Time-Varying Implicit Neural Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28047)
*Alper Sahistan,Haichao Miao,Zhimin Li,Peer-Timo Bremer,Joshua A Levine,Valerio Pascucci*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于delta tracking的查询高效随机体渲染框架，用于直接交互式渲染时间变化的隐式神经表示（INR）科学体积数据，在RTX 4090上达到1024x1024分辨率下约30-40 FPS的性能，时间步更新仅需1-2毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间变化的隐式神经表示（INR）是动态X射线CT等科学体积数据唯一实用的紧凑表示方式，但交互式体渲染INR极具挑战性，因为廉价的内存查找被昂贵的神经推理取代，传统的密集采样光行推进方法变得不切实际。虽然重采样、缓存和重训练可以缓解成本，但会牺牲便利性和准确性，且对时间变化数据不实用。

Method: 该系统采用基于delta tracking的查询高效随机体渲染框架，包含四阶段流水线，利用异构并行性：使用光线追踪核心（ray tracing cores）进行遍历，张量核心（tensor cores）进行批量神经评估。此外提出了射线预算（ray budgeting）和查询剪枝（query pruning）策略来减少INR查询次数，提升每帧性能。该系统可直接从原始表示渲染时间变化INR。

Result: 渲染器在RTX 4090 GPU上，1024x1024分辨率下达到约30-40 FPS，并收敛到高保真图像。同时支持连续域的交互式时间探索，时间步更新仅需约1-2毫秒。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了通过结合delta tracking、异构并行硬件利用以及查询优化策略，可以实现时间变化INR的直接交互式体渲染，兼顾性能、保真度和时间连续性探索能力。

Abstract: Time-varying implicit neural representations (INRs) provide a compact representation of scientific volumes and, for modalities such as dynamic X-ray computed tomography (CT), are often the only practical way to represent the data. However, interactive volume rendering of INRs is challenging, as cheap memory lookups are replaced by expensive neural inferences, hindering the performance. Therefore, conventional volume rendering methods such as ray marching with dense sampling are often impractical. While resampling, caching, and retraining can mitigate this cost, they compromise convenience and accuracy and become impractical for time-varying data. We tackle these challenges using a query-efficient stochastic volume rendering framework based on delta tracking. Our system employs a four-stage pipeline that exploits heterogeneous parallelism, using ray tracing cores for traversal and tensor cores for batched neural evaluation. Furthermore, we present strategies to reduce INR queries via ray budgeting and query pruning, thereby increasing per-frame performance. Using our renderer, many time-varying INRs can be rendered directly from their original representation. The system achieves ~30-40 FPS at 1024x1024 resolution on an RTX 4090 GPU and converges to high-fidelity images. Moreover, the system enables interactive temporal exploration of the continuous domain, with timestep updates taking approximately 1-2 ms.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Multi-Head Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27230)
*Cheng Luo,Zefan Cai,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多注意力头残差（MHAR）：将残差流中路由查询沿宽度维切分为H个逐子空间头，每个头对深度历史有独立softmax，使深度读取变为块对角结构。该方法零参数开销、计算量可忽略，在100M至1B规模上持续优于标准Transformer及多种基线，且头数H是一个真实设计轴（H=4或8为最优）。通过delta attention residual的恒等转换支持8B模型中期训练，在GSM8K和GPQA上分别提升+3.2和+3.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer通过单一加性残差流传播信息，Attention Residuals虽允许每个子层通过softmax读取深度历史，但使用单一查询共享整个宽度维度，即所有特征子空间必须通过同一个分布读取历史层。当不同子空间对"该读哪些层"的偏好不一致时（不一致性随模型宽度增长），这种被迫的妥协会带来越来越大的代价。因此需要一种让每个特征子空间拥有独立读取分布的方法。

Method: 将attention residuals中的路由查询重塑为H个逐子空间头，每个头拥有自己的softmax分布，覆盖深度历史。读取操作变为块对角结构。H=1时退化回标准attention residuals。实现采用Fused Triton路由内核，将训练吞吐量从baseline的0.2-0.5x提升至0.55-0.88x。此外，利用delta attention residuals设计恒等保持的转换方法，支持从已有模型进行8B中期训练。

Result: 在去重、质量过滤、STEM/代码加重型的Nemotron退火语料上，从零训练100M、350M、1B模型，验证损失分别比标准Transformer降低-0.061、-0.149和-0.140，且在四种方法中所有设置下均取得最佳结果，增益随规模增大而增加。验证损失对H呈U形曲线，最优为H=4或H=8，H=16过分割会损失部分增益。8B中期训练在GSM8K上+3.2、GPQA上+3.1。

Conclusion: MHAR证明头数是残差路由的真实设计维度而非免费旋钮，学习到的查询差异直接验证了子空间分歧是性能提升的根本驱动因素。该方法在多种规模下一致有效，且通过效率优化和恒等转换支持大规模模型的实际应用，为改进Transformer信息传播机制提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Transformers propagate information across depth through a single additive residual stream: every sublayer reads only the most recent state. Attention residuals relax this by letting each sublayer attend, through a learned softmax. However, that read uses a single query shared across the entire width, so every feature subspace must read the depth history through one distribution. The cost of this forced compromise grows with how much the subspaces disagree about which layers to read, and disagreement grows with model width. We introduce Multi-Head Attention Residuals (MHAR): the routing query is reshaped into H per-subspace heads, each with its own softmax over the depth history. The read becomes block-diagonal, the reshape adds zero parameters and negligible compute, and H = 1 recovers attention residuals exactly. Trained from scratch on a deduplicated Nemotron-based anneal corpus that is quality-filtered and STEM- and code-heavy, MHAR improves validation loss over a standard Transformer at 100M, 350M, and 1B (-0.061, -0.149, and -0.140). It achieves the best result among four methods in every setting, with the gain increasing from 100M to the larger scales. The head count is a real design axis rather than a free knob: validation loss is U-shaped with respect to H, with a flat optimum at H = 4 or H = 8 across scales. We adopt H = 8 for large-scale models; over-splitting beyond this point (H = 16) consistently gives back part of the gain. A direct probe of the trained queries confirms that learned subspace disagreement is the underlying driver. Fused Triton routing kernels increase attention-residual training throughput from 0.2-0.5x to 0.55-0.88x of the baseline while maintaining near-baseline peak memory. An identity-preserving conversion using delta attention residuals supports 8B mid-training, yielding improvements of +3.2 on GSM8K and +3.1 on GPQA.

</details>


### [176] [KernelGenBench: A Multi-Source and Multi-Chip Benchmark for LLM-based Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27231)
*Peiyu Zang,Jian Tao,Jialing Zhang,Yichen Yuan,Wentao Zhang,Guang Liu,Yonghua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了KernelGenBench，一个统一的基准测试，用于系统评估LLM和智能体生成的Triton内核，涵盖多算子来源（MS）和多硬件平台（MC）两个子基准。大规模评估（超过150亿token）揭示了三个关键发现：智能体方法优于纯LLM采样，跨硬件平台性能退化严重，以及自主内核生成的成本极其高昂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型增加了对高效加速器内核的需求，但内核开发仍是高度专业化和劳动密集型的工作。尽管LLM和智能体框架在自动内核生成方面取得进展，但仍缺乏一个全面的基准来跨多样化的算子来源和异构硬件平台严格评估LLM生成的内核质量。

Method: 提出了KernelGenBench基准，包含两个互补的子基准：(1) KernelGenBench-MS（多来源），从三个非标准PyTorch来源评估210个算子；(2) KernelGenBench-MC（多芯片），使用110个算子在六个异构硬件平台上评估性能可移植性。进行了超过150亿token的大规模评估。

Result: 评估结果表明：(1) 基于智能体的方法一致优于纯LLM采样方法，其中cuBLAS算子对所有方法最具挑战性；(2) 生成性能在不同硬件平台间差异显著，即使是近期内核专用智能体也遭受严重的跨平台性能退化（例如AutoKernel从NVIDIA上的87%降至Platform E上的25%）；(3) 自主内核生成成本极高，专用智能体方法平均每个成功算子消耗511万token（AKO4all达到519万），比简单LLM采样方法高出数量级。

Conclusion: KernelGenBench为LLM/智能体生成的内核提供了首个全面的跨来源、跨平台评估基准。当前研究揭示了智能体内核生成在性能、平台可移植性和成本效率方面的显著不足，表明该领域仍面临重大挑战，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have significantly increased the demand for efficient accelerator kernels, but kernel development remains a highly specialized and labor-intensive task. The recent rise of LLMs and agentic frameworks offers a promising pathway toward automatic kernel generation. However, despite rapid progress, there is still no comprehensive benchmark to rigorously evaluate LLM-generated kernels across diverse operator sources or heterogeneous hardware platforms. We present KernelGenBench, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating LLM- and agent-generated Triton kernels across diverse operator sources and heterogeneous hardware platforms. It comprises two complementary sub-benchmarks: KernelGenBench-MS (Multi-Source), evaluating 210 operators from three sources beyond standard PyTorch-centric tasks, and KernelGenBench-MC (Multi-Chip), measuring performance portability across six heterogeneous hardware platforms using a 110-operator subset. Our large-scale evaluation, consuming over 15 billion tokens, shows: (1) agent-based methods consistently outperform pure LLM sampling methods, while cuBLAS operators are the most challenging across all methods; (2) generation performance varies significantly across hardware platforms, with even recent kernel-specialized agents experiencing severe cross-platform degradation (e.g., AutoKernel drops from 87% on NVIDIA to 25% on Platform E); (3) autonomous kernel generation remains highly cost-intensive, with specialized agent methods averaging 5.11 million tokens per successful operator (AKO4all reaches 5.19 million), orders of magnitude higher than simple LLM sampling approaches.

</details>


### [177] [RadHarmony: Radiological Data Handling in the Era of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27235)
*Frank Li,Bardia Khosravi,Mohammadreza Chavoshi,Theo Dapamede,YoungSeok Jeon,Janice Newsome,Hari Trivedi,Judy Gichoya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RadHarmony是一个开源Python库，为放射学图像数据集提供统一的加载、协同处理和数据增强API，主要支持胸部X光片，并初步支持CT和MRI。它将24个公共数据集的元数据标准化为单一表格格式，包装MONAI数据集实现深度学习就绪的样本交付，通过统一接口支持分类标签、分割掩膜、边界框和放射学报告文本，并提供交互式可视化工具。该库还引入AI代理技能来引导新数据集的整合流程，并通过预训练RadHarmony-ViT参考模型（结合三个异构胸部X光数据集）展示了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放射学图像上训练深度学习模型需要整合来自不同来源、不同文件格式、目录布局、标签模式和注释类型的异构数据集，这一过程高度繁琐且缺乏标准化工具，迫切需要统一的解决方案来降低数据集整合的复杂性和门槛。

Method: RadHarmony通过以下方式实现：(1) 将24个公共数据集的元数据标准化为统一表格格式；(2) 包装MONAI的map-style数据集，提供深度学习就绪的样本交付并支持磁盘缓存；(3) 通过单一接口支持分类标签、分割掩膜、边界框和放射学报告文本等多种注释类型；(4) 提供交互式可视化工具用于数据集探索和验证；(5) 引入AI代理技能，引导从原始数据检查到代码生成和测试的完整整合流程。

Result: 通过预训练RadHarmony-ViT参考视觉Transformer基线模型，成功结合了三个异构胸部X光数据集且无需数据集特定代码，验证了该库在实际深度学习训练场景中的可用性和有效性。

Conclusion: RadHarmony为放射学影像数据集整合提供了统一的、低门槛的开源解决方案，通过AI代理技能进一步降低了新数据集接入的难度，代码和预训练模型权重已公开，为放射学AI研究提供了重要基础设施。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on radiological images requires integrating heterogeneous datasets across different sources, file formats, directory layouts, label schemas, and annotation types. We present RadHarmony, an open-source Python library that provides a unified API for loading, harmonizing, and augmenting radiological datasets, with a primary focus on chest radiographs and early support for computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). RadHarmony standardizes metadata from 24 public datasets into a single tabular format, wraps MONAI's map-style datasets for deep-learning-ready sample delivery with optional on-disk caching, and supports classification labels, segmentation masks, bounding boxes, and radiology report text through a single interface, with an interactive visualization tool for dataset exploration and verification. To lower the barrier for integrating new datasets, RadHarmony introduces an AI-agent skill that guides the full integration workflow from raw data inspection through code generation and testing. We demonstrate the library's utility by pretraining RadHarmony-ViT, a reference vision transformer baseline that combines three heterogeneous chest radiograph datasets with no dataset-specific code. The code and pretrained model weights are available at https://github.com/f10409/RadHarmony.

</details>


### [178] [Asymmetric Collapse in Model Merging: When Refusal Over- writes Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27240)
*Aarnav Choudhary,Matheus Fonseca Rocha,Jiwon Seo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过Gemma-3-1B-IT在两类互补安全任务（CARES伤害分级分类和WildJailbreak对抗性拒答）上的微调合并案例，发现标准模型合并方法（Linear、SLERP、TIES、DARE-TIES）在保留攻击抵抗力方面远优于分类准确性：合并后模型保留81-85%的越狱拒答率，但CARES分类准确率降至至多12.9%。研究表明这种不对称性并非由于任务向量方向冲突（二者余弦相似度仅0.011），而是因为拒答微调在不同层产生了一致更大的任务向量幅度，使对幅度敏感的合并方法偏向保留拒答更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并常用于无需额外训练即可组合不同微调模型的能力，但标准合并方法能否同时保留多个安全相关行为尚不明确。作者希望通过受控案例研究揭示合并方法在同时处理安全性分类和对抗性拒答等互补安全目标时的行为特性。

Method: 以Gemma-3-1B-IT为基础模型，在两个互补安全目标（CARES伤害级别分类与WildJailbreak对抗性拒答）上分别微调，然后使用Linear、SLERP、TIES和DARE-TIES四种标准合并方法融合两个微调模型，并在分类准确率、攻击抵抗力和良性合规性三个维度上评估合并后模型的性能，同时通过权重空间分析（任务向量方向和幅度）来探究不对称性背后的机理。

Result: 四种合并方法均表现出攻击抵抗力的转移显著优于分类准确率：合并模型保留81-85%的越狱拒答率，而CARES分类准确率下降至至多12.9%。任务向量近似正交（余弦相似度0.011），排除方向冲突的解释；拒答微调在各层产生一致更大的任务向量幅度，导致对幅度敏感的合并方法偏向保留拒答更新。

Conclusion: 标准模型合并可能将安全识别能力坍缩为广泛拒答行为，当安全相关任务向量在幅度上差异显著时尤其如此。这一发现对依赖模型合并来组合多重安全能力（如精细分类与对抗性拒答）的实践提出了警示。

Abstract: Model merging is often used to combine capabilities from separately fine-tuned models without additional training, but it is unclear whether standard merging methods preserve multiple safety-relevant behaviors simultaneously. We study this question through a controlled case study using two Gemma-3-1B-IT finetunes on two complementary safety objectives: CARES harm-level classification and WildJailbreak adversarial refusal. We merge the two fine-tunes using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and DARE-TIES, and evaluate the merged models on classification accuracy, attack resistance, and benign compliance. Across all four methods, attack resistance transfers significantly more than classification accuracy: merged models retain 81-85% jailbreak refusal rates while CARES accuracy falls to at most 12.9%. Weight-space measurements suggest that this asymmetry is not caused by strongly opposing task-vector directions: the two task vectors are nearly orthogonal (cosine similarity 0.011). Instead, the refusal fine-tune induces consistently larger per-layer task-vector magnitudes, causing magnitude-sensitive methods to favor refusal updates. These results show that standard model merging can collapse safety recognition into broad refusal when safety-relevant task vectors differ substantially in scale.

</details>


### [179] [Divergence Decoding: Training-Free Capability Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27248)
*Yimi Wang,Hao Li,Shuo Yang,He Cao,Dechen Zhang,Ziang Wu,Zhiyuan Yan,Fanyang Mo,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Divergence Decoding，一个无需训练的推理时框架，通过监测通用大模型与领域专家模型在每个token上的Jensen-Shannon散度差异，动态路由控制权，实现通用推理能力与领域专业知识的融合，在多个科学基准上超越单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型存在一个两难困境：通用模型推理能力强但缺乏专业知识，领域专家模型虽知识丰富却存在推理能力下降和鲁棒性降低等专化副作用。现有方法无法在不重新训练的情况下兼得两者优势。

Method: Divergence Decoding将投机解码的"草稿-验证"框架重构为自适应路由机制。核心思想是使用Jensen-Shannon散度监测通用模型与专家模型在每个token上的分布分歧程度。当专家模型产生显著分歧时，该方法将其识别为潜在推理风险，并即时将控制权路由至通用模型，从而实现动态注入通用推理能力的同时保留领域专长，完成推理时的策略组合。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama系列模型上，针对GPQA、ChemBench和ChemCoTBench等具有挑战性的科学基准进行评估，Divergence Decoding在领域专化模型和通用模型上均表现出更优性能，超越大多数单一模型基线。

Conclusion: Divergence Decoding提供了一种通用的、无需训练的范式，通过自适应推理时协作来融合不同LLM的能力，有效解决了通用模型与专家模型之间的能力权衡问题。

Abstract: While large language models excel in reasoning, these generalists often lack knowledge for specialized scientific domains. Conversely, domain models~(specialists), while knowledgeable, suffer from specialization side-effects including diminished logic and reduced robustness.To address this dilemma, we introduce Divergence Decoding, a training-free framework for capability fusion. It reconstructs the "draft-and-verify" skeleton of speculative decoding into an adaptive routing mechanism. The core is using Jensen-Shannon divergence to monitor the distributional disagreement between the two models at each token. When the specialist exhibits significant divergence, our method identifies it as a potential reasoning risk and instantaneously routes control to the generalist. This allows the dynamic injection of general reasoning while preserving domain expertise, achieving inference-time policy composition of the generalist and the specialist.We evaluate Divergence Decoding across diverse model families (Qwen and Llama series) on challenging scientific benchmarks (GPQA, ChemBench, and ChemCoTBench). Experimental results demonstrate that Divergence Decoding outperforms both the domain-specialized and general-purpose models, effectively surpassing the performance of most single-model baseline. This suggests that Divergence Decoding provides a general, training-free paradigm for fusing diverse LLM capabilities through adaptive inference-time collaboration.

</details>


### [180] [PIE-APT: A Unified Framework for Temporal Planning and Contradiction Hunting via Incremental Direct-Derivation Abduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27287)
*Amir Hossein Sharafi,Alireza Shahbazi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，包含PIE-Abducer（增量直接推导式溯因）和PIE-APT（时序知识图谱上的溯因规划）两个集成模块，在描述逻辑（DL）上原生操作，通过非单调更新建模线性时间线上的状态转移，解决开放世界环境下动态知识图谱推理与规划中的不完全信息问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态知识图谱上的推理与规划在开放世界、信息不完全环境中面临重大挑战。现有动作形式化方法常遇到可判定性问题与Ramification Problem，而通过结构性溯因管理不完全知识需要进行指数级组合搜索，效率低下。

Method: 框架由两个模块组成：(1) PIE-Abducer采用增量直接推导式溯因，通过向一致分支注入目标公式的否定，从直接反驳结论中提取缺失前提，规避传统最小命中集（MHS）枚举；(2) PIE-APT采用递归的Generate-and-Test架构，将反向链A*搜索与PIE-Abducer交错进行至有界因果深度，再通过前向链时序投影严格验证。动作以OWL原生表示，无需外部模态算子，增量推理器作为黑盒使用以保持逻辑可判定性。

Result: 在四个OWL基准上评估，涵盖经典规划中不存在的语义能力（参数化目标见证搜索、搜索中途的DL蕴含、开放世界假设注入、对抗性矛盾寻找），结果表明该方法在定性上优于经典规划器，且直接推导式方法在溯因丰富过程中定量上优于MHS基线。

Conclusion: 该框架证明在描述逻辑原生环境中可以实现开放世界动态知识图谱上的可判定溯因规划，直接推导式溯因相比MHS枚举在组合搜索效率上具有显著优势。

Abstract: Reasoning and planning over Dynamic Knowledge Graphs (DKGs) present significant challenges, especially in open-world environments with incomplete information. Existing action formalisms often face decidability issues and the Ramification Problem, while managing incomplete knowledge via structural abduction requires expansive combinatorial search. This paper introduces a unified framework with two integrated modules---\textbf{PIE-Abducer} (incremental direct-derivation abduction) and \textbf{PIE-APT} (Abductive Planning for Temporal KGs)---operating natively on the highly expressive Description Logic. We model state transitions along a linear timeline as non-monotonic updates to deductively closed DL theories. Treating the incremental reasoner as a black-box and representing actions natively in OWL without external modal operators preserves logical decidability. To address incomplete knowledge, \textbf{PIE-Abducer} circumvents traditional Minimal Hitting Set (MHS) enumeration. Instead of combinatorial syntactic search, it injects the logical negation of a target goal into a consistent branch and extracts missing premises via direct refutation consequences. \textbf{PIE-APT} then employs a recursive \textit{Generate-and-Test} architecture, interleaving backward-chaining A* search with \textbf{PIE-Abducer} up to a bounded causal depth, followed by strict validation via forward-chaining Temporal Projection. We evaluate four OWL benchmarks stressing semantic abilities absent in classical planning: parameterized goals with witness search, mid-search DL entailment, open-world assumption injection, and adversarial contradiction hunting. Results demonstrate qualitative superiority over classical planners and prove our direct-derivation approach quantitatively outperforms an MHS-faithful baseline during abductive enrichment.

</details>


### [181] [PAUSE: A User-Centric Benchmark for Personal AI Assistants in Unified Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27354)
*Haoyu Chen,Xirui Shi,Yuyao Wang,Jerry Chen,Di Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAUSE，一个面向个人AI助手的用户中心基准测试，用于评估智能体在状态化、多服务集成环境中的表现。基准涵盖跨异构用户资源的协调、状态一致性维护、授权约束下的多轮交互，并采用多机制评估框架。实验表明最先进的专有模型在需要状态推理和配置感知的场景中任务完成率不足70%，同时提出了可扩展的用户中心合成数据生成流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试往往将服务上下文碎片化或抽象掉用户状态，无法充分评估个人AI助手在真实服务场景下需要处理持久用户状态、尊重用户配置与权限、以及维持长期约束感知交互的核心能力。

Method: PAUSE基准包含：(1)状态化的服务集成环境，要求智能体协调跨异构用户拥有的资源；(2)显式的用户-智能体交互通过真实的用户模拟实现；(3)多机制评估框架——开放性服务管理任务采用语义和轨迹级行为指标，约束密集型任务采用确定性的基于状态的验证；(4)用户中心合成数据生成流程，可规模化地产生一致的服务环境、用户配置和带可靠标注的任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，即使是最先进的专有模型在需要状态推理和配置感知的场景中任务完成率也无法达到70%，并揭示了一致且可解释的失败模式。

Conclusion: 当前模型在真实个人助理部署场景中仍存在显著不足，PAUSE基准及其合成数据生成流程为未来的基准扩展和用户中心个人AI助手研究提供了坚实的基础。

Abstract: Personal AI assistants are increasingly deployed as task-oriented, tool-augmented agents that operate within unified service environments to support everyday user activities. In realistic settings, such assistants must reason over persistent user state, respect user-specific configurations and permissions, and sustain long-horizon, constraint-aware interactions across multiple services. Existing benchmarks, however, often fragment service contexts or abstract away user state, limiting their ability to evaluate user-centric personal assistant behavior in realistic service settings. We introduce PAUSE, a user-centric benchmark for evaluating personal AI assistants in stateful, service-integrated environments. PAUSE captures core challenges of real-world assistant deployment by requiring agents to coordinate actions across heterogeneous user-owned resources while maintaining consistency with environment state, authorization constraints over multi-turn interactions. The benchmark incorporates explicit user-agent interaction via realistic user simulation, enabling evaluation beyond static tool execution. To support principled and reproducible evaluation, PAUSE adopts a multi-regime evaluation framework aligned with task characteristics. Open-ended service management tasks are assessed using semantic and trajectory-level behavioral metrics, while constraint-intensive tasks admit deterministic, state-based verification. Benchmark results show that even state-of-the-art proprietary models fail to reach 70% task completion on scenarios requiring stateful reasoning and configuration awareness, revealing consistent and interpretable failure patterns. Finally, we present a user-centric synthesis pipeline that enables scalable generation of coherent service environments, user configurations, and reliably annotated tasks, supporting benchmark extensibility and future research.

</details>


### [182] [SkillMentor: LLM Agent Self-Evolution via Learning Blind-Spot Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27360)
*Xiaoyi Bao,Yuanzhen Xie,Yunzhi Tan,Jinghang Gu,Zhongqing Wang,Chu-Ren Huang,Bo Hu,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillMentor框架，将"盲点诊断"作为独立于执行的智能体能力进行研究。通过强化学习训练Mentor策略，使其能够生成诊断任务、识别重复失败模式并将其整理为可复用的纠正技能。在AppWorld和BFCLv3基准上，平均提升执行器性能44.2%，证明了盲点诊断是可学习的能力，且无需更新执行器权重或依赖人工标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体自我进化研究主要关注"如何行动"的学习，而忽视了"发现自身未知之处"的能力。已有方法通常假设失败发现是给定的，仅专注于识别失败后的修复。作者提出质疑：盲点诊断本身是否可以被学习？这一能力与执行分离，属于被现有研究忽视的独立能力维度。

Method: 将诊断作为独立于执行的智能体能力进行研究，排除执行器适应和人工监督这两种替代性进展来源，确保所有改进均源自学习到的诊断能力。提出SkillMentor框架，通过强化学习训练Mentor策略，使其完成三件事：生成诊断任务、识别重复失败模式、将失败模式整理为可复用的纠正技能。

Result: 在AppWorld和BFCLv3两个基准上，SkillMentor平均提升执行器性能44.2%。该结果表明盲点诊断是可学习的独立能力，能够在无需更新执行器权重或依赖人工策划数据的情况下实现自我进化。

Conclusion: 盲点诊断是智能体自我进化中可学习的独立能力，SkillMentor通过强化学习训练诊断策略而非更新执行器，即可显著提升整体性能，为智能体自我进化提供了新的方向——不仅要学会"如何做"，更要学会"知道自己不知道什么"。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution has primarily focused on learning how to act, while overlooking an equally important capability: learning to discover what an agent does not know. Existing approaches typically assume that failure discovery is given, focusing on how to repair failures once they are identified. We ask whether blind-spot diagnosis itself can be learned. We thus study diagnosis as an agent capability separate from execution, and exclude two alternative sources of progress: executor adaptation and human supervision. Under these constraints, performance cannot improve through executor updates or annotated examples, forcing all improvements to originate from the learned diagnostic capability. We propose SkillMentor, which trains a Mentor policy via reinforcement learning to generate diagnostic tasks, identify recurrent failure modes, and curate them into reusable corrective skills. Across AppWorld and BFCLv3, SkillMentor improves executor performance by an average of 44.2%. These results suggest that blind-spot diagnosis is a learnable capability, enabling self-evolution without updating executor weights or relying on human-curated data.

</details>


### [183] [Beyond the Bidirectional Promise: Re-evaluating the Robustness of Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27386)
*Saurabh Yadav,Badri Narayana Patro,Vijay Srinivas Agneeswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了扩散语言模型（DLM）在自然输入噪声和对抗攻击下的鲁棒性与校准性能，与自回归（AR）基线进行两两参数匹配对比（LLaDA-8B vs. LLaMA-3-8B，Dream-7B vs. Qwen2.5-7B），覆盖32种自然扰动条件、对抗梯度探针和机制性隐状态分析。研究发现DLM的高随机性损失景观天然抵抗基于梯度的对抗后缀，但对自然噪声无固有防御；DLM表现出系统性过度自信；机制探针揭示所有模型都能完美编码输入损坏，行为脆弱性完全源于解码器路由失败，因此表面提示修补无法改善噪声基线，鲁棒性必须内在集成到迭代解码循环中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）作为自回归生成的有力替代方案，具备双向上下文和迭代精炼能力，但其在自然输入噪声和对抗攻击下的可靠性尚未得到充分探索。作者旨在填补这一研究空白，通过严格配对实验设计区分架构固有属性与权重依赖行为，并诊断DLM鲁棒性问题的根本机制。

Method: 采用参数匹配的配对模型设计（LLaDA-8B vs. LLaMA-3-8B，Dream-7B vs. Qwen2.5-7B），系统性地在32种自然扰动条件下评估鲁棒性和校准性能；使用对抗梯度探针测试损失景观对攻击的敏感性；通过机制性隐状态分析（mechanistic hidden-state probing）检查模型内部对输入损坏的编码程度。

Result: 发现DLM呈现细微的鲁棒性画像：其高度随机的损失景观天然抵抗基于梯度的对抗后缀攻击，但无法对自然噪声提供保证性防御，说明日常鲁棒性是权重依赖的而非架构固有的；DLM表现出系统性过度置信，构成实际部署隐患；机制探针显示所有模型都能完美编码输入损坏，行为脆弱性完全由解码器路由失败导致，且表面提示修补无法改善噪声基线。

Conclusion: DLM的鲁棒性无法通过表面修补（prompt patching）获得改善，必须从根本上将其集成到迭代解码循环中设计。即鲁棒性应作为DLM架构的内在组成部分，而非事后补救手段。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer a compelling alternative to autoregressive (AR) generation by enabling bidirectional context and iterative refinement. However, their reliability under natural input noise and adversarial attacks remains under-explored. To address this, we systematically evaluate DLM robustness and calibration against AR baselines, using two parameter-matched pairs (LLaDA-8B vs. LLaMA-3-8B and Dream-7B vs. Qwen2.5-7B) across 32 natural perturbation conditions, adversarial gradient probes, and mechanistic hidden-state analyses. This paired design effectively isolates architecture-intrinsic properties from weight-dependent behaviors. We find a nuanced robustness profile: while highly stochastic DLM loss landscapes naturally resist gradient-based adversarial suffixes, they provide no guaranteed defense against natural noise, proving that everyday robustness is weight-dependent rather than inherently architectural. Furthermore, DLMs exhibit systematic overconfidence, presenting a practical deployment hazard. Most crucially, mechanistic probing reveals that all models perfectly encode input corruption, isolating behavioral fragility entirely to a decoder routing failure. Consistent with this diagnosis, we show that surface-level prompt patching fails to improve over noisy baselines. Ultimately, DLM robustness cannot be patched on; it must be fundamentally integrated into the iterative decoding loop.

</details>


### [184] [Bridging Inference-Time Scaling and Episodic Memory with Action-Centric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27415)
*Xu Zheng,Chaohao Lin,Zhuomin Chen,Weijieying Ren,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GAMER框架，通过构建基于动作的图结构记忆与情景推理，将推理时缩放与情景记忆相结合，解耦记忆机制与LLM，并通过双流时序差分学习估计动作节点的正负价值，实现双向优化决策，显著提升Agent推理效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时缩放方法在Agent场景下以无状态方式运行，导致冗余搜索，效率低下；已有的记忆机制严重依赖LLM的推理能力，计算成本高昂。作者希望弥合推理缩放与情景记忆之间的鸿沟，降低token/金钱消耗。

Method: 提出GAMER框架：(1) 将历史推理建模为动态的动作中心图（Action-Centric Graph），将记忆机制从LLM中解耦，减少记忆上下文从而节省token/金钱开销；(2) 使用双流时序差分（Temporal Difference）学习机制，分别估计动作节点的正价值（建议）和负价值（规避），基于历史成功与失败经验；(3) 推理阶段利用学习到的价值函数进行双向决策优化：正价值提供行动建议，负价值标记高危动作；(4) 在图上进行高效搜索，提升推理缩放效率。

Result: 在多个benchmark上的实验表明，相比vanilla基线，GAMER在成功率和进展率上分别取得了20.81%和6.17%的相对提升，同时相比记忆机制baseline使用更少的记忆上下文。

Conclusion: GAMER通过将记忆机制与LLM解耦并以图结构建模历史推理，有效提升了Agent在推理时缩放场景下的效率与性能，为推理缩放与情景记忆的结合提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Recent advancements in inference-time scaling have significantly unlocked the complex reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models~(LLMs). However, for agents, these approaches suffer from a critical inefficiency, operating in a stateless manner and engaging in redundant search processes. Existing memory mechanisms largely rely on the reasoning capabilities of LLMs, leading to prohibitive computational costs. In this paper, we propose a novel framework, \textit{GAMER}~(Graph-based Action-centric Memory with Episodic Reasoning), that bridges the gap between inference scaling and episodic memory. Our approach models historical reasoning as a dynamic \textit{Action-Centric Graph}. By decoupling the memory mechanism from LLMs, our method can save token/money usage by providing less memory context than memory mechanism baselines. To extract knowledge from the graph effectively, we use a dual-stream Temporal Difference learning mechanism to estimate the positive~(suggestion) and negative~(avoidance) value of action nodes based on past successes and failures. During the inference phase, this learned value function optimizes decision-making bi-directionally, so that positive values provide action suggestions, while negative values indicate high-risk actions. By performing efficient searches on the graph, our method significantly improves the efficiency of inference scaling. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that \textit{GAMER} achieves superior performance by \textbf{20.81\%/6.17\%} for success/progress rate compared to vanilla baselines.

</details>


### [185] [Dimensionality and Measurement Precision in HLE's Multiple-Choice Subset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27420)
*Mayank Sharma,Savira Nadela,Tyler Matteson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用心理测量学方法对Humanity's Last Exam (HLE)基准进行了实证评估，发现该基准测量的是单一通用推理因子（ω_h = 0.998），领域标签仅解释3.5%的项目反应方差，且测量精度集中在中等能力水平，对最强大模型的区分能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLE被广泛用于评估前沿语言模型，其八个学科领域的子分数常被解读为不同能力的证据。然而，尚无研究验证这些标签是否对应经验上可分离的潜在构念，也未评估该基准对能力相近模型的有效区分度。

Method: 评估29个LLM在HLE文本多项选择子集（428个项目）上的表现，采用双参数逻辑斯蒂克IRT模型进行分析，包括McDonald's ω_h检验单维性、领域标签对项目反应方差的解释比例、域内/域间残差相关性对比，以及测试信息函数分析测量精度分布。

Result: HLE测量单一通用推理因子（ω_h = 0.998），领域标签仅解释3.5%的方差，域内与域间残差相关性几乎相同（Cohen's d = 0.016），领域特定能力估计与总分高度相关（r ≥ 0.81）。测量精度集中在中等能力水平，在θ=0以上急剧下降，而前沿模型恰处于该区间。

Conclusion: HLE的领域子分数不构成独立能力解释的充分证据，且该基准对最强大模型之间的区分能力有限。

Abstract: Humanity's Last Exam (HLE) is widely used to evaluate frontier language models. HLE organizes its questions into eight subject-domain categories, whose subscores are often interpreted as evidence of distinct capabilities. However, no study has assessed whether these labels correspond to empirically separable latent constructs, nor whether the benchmark effectively differentiates between models of similar ability. We evaluate 29 LLMs on the text-only multiple-choice subset of HLE ($J = 428$ items) and apply psychometric methods to assess both the dimensionality of the benchmark and the distribution of its measurement precision. Fitting a two-parameter logistic IRT model, we find convergent evidence that HLE measures a single general reasoning factor: McDonald's $ω_h = 0.998$, domain labels explain only 3.5\% of item response variance, within- and between-domain residual correlations are nearly identical (Cohen's $d = 0.016$), and domain-specific ability estimates are near-redundant with the total score ($r \geq 0.81$). A separate analysis of the test information function reveals that measurement precision concentrates at moderate ability levels and drops sharply above $θ= 0$, where frontier models sit. These findings suggest that HLE's domain subscores do not warrant distinct capability interpretations and that the benchmark's ability to discriminate among the strongest models is limited.

</details>


### [186] [Leveraging Trajectory Graphs for Pre-Execution Error Diagnosis in Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27443)
*Xu Zheng,Zhuomin Chen,Chaohao Lin,Hua Wei,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Trajectory Graph Copilot框架，通过图神经网络建模历史轨迹为概率图，在动作执行前诊断潜在错误，作为LLM智能体的"副驾驶"实现事前纠错，在四个基准上平均提升14.69%的通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长时序交互任务（如具身AI）中表现不佳，主要由于巨大的动作空间和任务复杂性导致复合误差，单个次优动作可能使整个轨迹偏离，耗尽有限的步数预算。现有的微调方案代价高昂，因此需要一种无需昂贵微调的事前错误诊断方法。

Method: 提出Trajectory Graph Copilot框架，核心组件为Graph Debugger：将历史轨迹建模为概率图，利用图神经网络（GNN）识别频繁导致失败的序列动作模式。该框架作为主动诊断沙盒，在动作执行前提供早期预警，提示智能体自我纠正。

Result: 在四个基准测试上使用三种LLM智能体进行广泛实验，平均通过率（pass ratio）提升14.69%。

Conclusion: 事前动作错误诊断（pre-action error diagnosis）能够有效防止代价高昂的错误，显著增强LLM智能体完成长时序任务的能力，为长时序交互任务提供了一种无需微调的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model~(LLM)-based agents have demonstrated exceptional performance across a wide range of complex interactive tasks. However, they often struggle with long-horizon interactive tasks common in domains, such as embodied AI. The complexity and vast action spaces in these settings lead to compounding errors, where a single suboptimal action can derail an entire trajectory, causing the agent to exhaust its limited step budget on inefficient or unrecoverable paths. To overcome this without costly fine-tuning, we draw inspiration from software debugging, where execution logs are analyzed to preemptively catch errors. We propose \textit{Trajectory Graph Copilot}, a novel framework that acts as a ``copilot'' for LLM agents by diagnosing potential action errors before they are executed. At its core,\textit{Graph Debugger} models historical trajectories as a probabilistic graph and uses a Graph Neural Network to identify sequential action patterns that frequently lead to failure. Functioning as a proactive diagnostic sandbox, our method provides early warnings on potentially flawed actions, prompting the agent to self-correct. This pre-action error diagnosis prevents costly mistakes, significantly enhancing the agent's ability to complete long-horizon tasks successfully. The extensive experiments on four benchmarks with three LLM agents demonstrate a $14.69\%$ pass ratio improvement on average.

</details>


### [187] [VAmoS Bench: Voice Agent Simulation Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27453)
*Joshua Meyer,Sahar Shayegan,Ritiz Tambi,Ali Khan,Sun Kim,Victor Shih,Mehdi Jamei,Andi Partovi*

Main category: cs.AI
